## From the Author to the Reader

Before you begin reading this document, I would like to tell you where Evertophy came from. The shortest way to understand what it says is to understand what it is looking for.

### Not a claim to an answer, but a search for one

Modern science is the most reliable structure of knowledge humanity has ever built. It rests on findings that have been tested by experiment, confirmed by observation and measured again and again. Yet when we look at this structure closely, we see that some of its pieces do not quite fit together.

We measure the effect of most of the matter and energy in the universe, but we do not know what it is; what we call "dark matter" and "dark energy" are, in a sense, names for our ignorance. Many of the fundamental equations of physics work the same way forwards and backwards in "time", yet in the world we live in time has a direction. Quantum mechanics works with extraordinary accuracy, but there is still no agreement on what a measurement "is". General relativity and quantum theory are each flawless in their own domain, but they do not come together. And the question "what was there before the Big Bang?" stands side by side with the debate over whether the question itself is meaningful.

These are not failures of science; they are its present limits. The measurement is there, the experiment is there. What is missing is the meaning that connects those measurements to one another.

Evertophy was born from these gaps. But I want to be clear from the outset: Evertophy does not claim to be the answer to these questions. Evertophy is a **search for an answer**: an attempt to look at poorly understood phenomena, and at scientific findings that appear as anomalies, from within a coherent framework that might connect them.

### Basic principles: "Experiment, Measurement and Observation" are accepted unconditionally; "Postulate and Interpretation" are marked as optional

Evertophy's search for meaning and solutions obeys one basic rule, and every line of this document is bound to it:

$$\boxed{\;\text{The outputs of Experiment, Measurement and Observation are accepted UNCONDITIONALLY; postulate and interpretation layers are evaluated as OPTIONAL – INTERPRETATION-FREE.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{The outputs of Experiment, Measurement and Observation are accepted UNCONDITIONALLY; postulate and interpretation layers are evaluated as OPTIONAL – INTERPRETATION-FREE.}\;}
```

This means the following. Evertophy rejects no measurement. It declares no experimental result "wrong", and it bends no observation to fit its own framework. If a measurement contradicts an Evertophy reading, it is the reading that must change, not the measurement.

In scientific reality, "the measurement itself" and "the interpretation built on the measurement" are not the same thing. The track a particle leaves in a detector is a measurement; whether that track is a "wave-function collapse", a "branching of worlds" or something else is an interpretation. That galaxies rotate faster than expected is an observation; whether this comes from an invisible particle or from gravitation itself behaving differently is an interpretation.

Evertophy approaches these layers in three ways:

1. **Optional:** It treats none of the existing interpretations as mandatory, and rejects none of them. It keeps them all on the table as options.
2. **Interpretation-free:** It loads no interpretation onto the measurement itself. It takes the raw finding as it is and places its own reading on top of it as a separate layer. The reading Evertophy proposes is also an option; it is a candidate to be tested alongside the others, not to replace them.
3. **Physical reality and mathematics:** The mathematical flawlessness of a theory or hypothesis does not, by itself, count as physical reality (experiment, measurement, observation); such a theory has the same standing as the two items above. By contrast, a mathematical representation that correctly reproduces a measurement is accepted unconditionally within the scope of that measurement. The same measurement may have more than one correct representation — the same orbit can be written in the language of Newton and in that of general relativity — and none of these representations is ontologically prior to another.

$$\boxed{\;\text{Measurement: unconditional}\qquad\text{Representation: valid within the scope in which it reproduces the measurement}\qquad\text{Ontological priority among representations: none}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{Measurement: unconditional}\qquad\text{Representation: valid within the scope in which it reproduces the measurement}\qquad\text{Ontological priority among representations: none}\;}
```

Seen from this perspective, Evertophy readings should be evaluated as follows. Evertophy unconditionally accepts the entire scope of General Relativity, the Standard Model, Quantum Mechanics and Cosmology that rests on experiment, measurement and observation. It does not put itself in the place of any model or theorem. Evertophy holds the status of a **Research Programme / Candidate Encompassing Framework** that seeks to be a "Rosetta Stone" for bringing together the missing pieces of science — a common translation grammar for existing scientific frameworks that speak in different languages.

### The search for meaning: joining the disconnected pieces of science

How, then, does Evertophy try to join these pieces?

The core Evertophy proposal is this: reality does not consist of a single plane, nor only of particles made of matter; it unfolds in a **THREE-PHASE** structure. The whole of the structure formed by the three phases is called the **Dynamic Universe** ($U_x$). The principle that holds these three phases together, and that underlies every interaction among them, is the **Force of Attraction**; it is Evertophy's most fundamental ontological postulate (Section 1.2). The four forces we know — Gravitation, the Strong Nuclear Force, the Weak Nuclear Force and the Electromagnetic Force — are different projections of the Force of Attraction **according to scale, amount of mass and phase**.

$$\boxed{\;\{\text{Gravitation},\ \text{Strong},\ \text{Weak},\ \text{Electromagnetism}\} \;=\; \Pi_{\text{scale, mass, phase}}\big[\,\text{Force of Attraction}\,\big]\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\{\text{Gravitation},\ \text{Strong},\ \text{Weak},\ \text{Electromagnetism}\} \;=\; \Pi_{\text{scale, mass, phase}}\big[\,\text{Force of Attraction}\,\big]\;}
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $\Pi_{\text{scale, mass, phase}}$ | Projection: the form the Force of Attraction takes at a given scale, amount of mass and phase; its formal definition is open |
| Force of Attraction | Fundamental ontological postulate; it is not gravitation — gravitation is one of its projections in Cₘ |

![Core Ontology](Evertophy_Core_20260919_revize_gerekiyor_v2.png)

### How to read the figure

The figure above shows the entire ontological skeleton of Evertophy in a single picture. The picture consists of two levels, and the two levels must not be confused with each other.

**Inner level — the phase level.** Inside the central rectangle there are three nodes: at the top **c₀** (Potential), at the lower left **Cₘ** (Matter/Time), at the lower right **c⁺** (Upper Phase / Coordination). These three nodes form a **complete triangle** (K3), in which each is directly connected to the other two. The edges of the triangle carry the names of the transitions:

| Edge | Phases it connects | Record (stamp) behaviour | Observability from Cₘ |
|---|---|---|---|
| $\Delta_{0m}$ | c₀ ↔ Cₘ | Carries a stamp | Direct |
| $\Delta_{+m}$ | c⁺ ↔ Cₘ | Carries a stamp | Indirect |
| $\Delta_{0+}$ | c₀ ↔ c⁺ | Produces no stamp (does not touch Cₘ) | Not observed |

The fact that the arrows on the edges are **double-headed** is deliberate: phase transitions are bidirectional on all three edges. What is irreversible is not the transition itself, but the record that is produced in Cₘ during the transition and sealed in c⁺ (Sections 13–14). Drawing c₀ at the top of the figure is only a drawing convention; it does not mean a hierarchy or a centre. From the point of view of measurement and record, the centre is Cₘ, but topologically Cₘ is not a mandatory intermediate stop: there is a direct edge between c₀ and c⁺.

$$\boxed{\;c_0\leftrightarrow C_m,\qquad C_m\leftrightarrow c^+,\qquad c_0\leftrightarrow c^+\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;c_0\leftrightarrow C_m,\qquad C_m\leftrightarrow c^+,\qquad c_0\leftrightarrow c^+\;}
```

**The rectangle — the Dynamic Universe ($U_x$).** The whole that the three phases form together is $U_x$. $U_x$ is not a fourth phase, nor a node added to the triangle; it is the name of the holistic structure in which the three phases are realized together. Time, space and measurement live **inside** this whole, in the Cₘ phase.

**Outer level — the global level.** To the left of the rectangle is **$U_0$** (Absolute Core), to its right **$U_+$** (Absolute Dissolution). These two red circles are not phases; they are the two **limiting conditions** of the Dynamic Universe as a whole. This is why the curly brackets are drawn in the figure: the bidirectional arrows of $U_0$ and $U_+$ connect not to any vertex of the triangle but to the **whole** enclosed by the brackets.

$$\boxed{\;U_0\;\leftrightarrow\;U_x\;\leftrightarrow\;U_+,\qquad U_0,\ U_x,\ U_+\notin\{c_0,\ C_m,\ c^+\}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;U_0\;\leftrightarrow\;U_x\;\leftrightarrow\;U_+,\qquad U_0,\ U_x,\ U_+\notin\{c_0,\ C_m,\ c^+\}\;}
```

This distinction matters: the formation of a single black hole (a region passing into the c₀ regime) is a local phase event, and it happens in the universe every day. $U_0$, by contrast, is a global condition belonging to the **entire** Dynamic Universe. Likewise, c⁺ is a phase and lies inside the universe; $U_+$ is a limiting condition of the whole and lies outside it. The global arrows make no claim of mathematical reversibility; they show the two ends of a relation. At the global level there is no metric, no space and no time; for this reason the language of "before", "after" or "speed" is not used there.

### Ontological explanations of the figure's symbols

---

* **$U_0$ (Universe Core — Absolute Core):** The pre-layer in which all the structures that make up the Dynamic Universe we live in — c₀ (black holes), Cₘ (baryonic matter) and c⁺ (the dark sector: dark matter and dark energy) — exist in **a single structural form**, contiguous to a degree that converges to the metric-gapless state at the Planck scale; that is, the "fundamental source" structure.
  * In the $U_0$ regime, three-dimensional Euclidean space, baryonic matter, dark matter and dark energy, and time **do not exist, but are potentially present**. With the Big Bang, it brings the three phases — c₀, Cₘ and c⁺ — into existence **together, in the same constitution**; this constitution also gives birth to the **time** we experience as a unidirectional flow in the matter phase (emergent time; Section 7). This joint birth is not a sequential production: $U_0$ does not produce one first and then the other; all three are born at once.

  $$\boxed{\;U_0\;\rightsquigarrow\;\{c_0,\;C_m,\;c^+\}_{\text{together, in the same constitution}}\;}$$

  ```latex
  \boxed{\;U_0\;\rightsquigarrow\;\{c_0,\;C_m,\;c^+\}_{\text{together, in the same constitution}}\;}
  ```

  * When **all** the structures making up the Dynamic Universe have collapsed into the black-hole phase — that is, when the whole of $U_x$ reaches the critical saturation threshold ($\Theta_0$) — the state is represented by $U_0$. When this threshold is crossed, the Big Bang takes place and a new $U_x$ (Dynamic Universe) is formed: three-dimensional Euclidean space, baryonic matter and the dark sector are established anew. The threshold is not $U_0$ itself; it lies at the **boundary** where the collapse end of $U_x$ touches $U_0$ (Sections 2.6, 2.8).
  * $U_0$ is not a phase, nor a universe-scale subtype of c₀; there is no ordinary phase gate leading to or from it. The c₀ regime of a single black hole is local; $U_0$ is the global condition of the whole universe. No numerical mass value is assigned to $U_0$ (Section 2.10).

* **$U_+$ (Dissolved Universe — Absolute Dissolution / Absolute Rupture):** The limiting state in which the entire phase-structural resolution of the Dynamic Universe thins out until it crosses the global resolution limit. If $U_0$ is the global saturation pole, $U_+$ is the global dissolution pole. The names "Absolute Dissolution" and "Absolute Rupture" describe **the same limiting state**: the first emphasizes the state itself, the second the whole $U_x$ structure reaching that state. The event that takes place when the whole of $U_x$ crosses the critical threshold at the boundary of $U_+$ is called the **Big Rupture**; a new $U_x$ is then formed.

  $$\begin{cases} U_x \text{ crosses the critical threshold at the boundary of } U_0 & \Rightarrow\ \textbf{Big Bang} \\[4pt] U_x \text{ crosses the critical threshold at the boundary of } U_+ & \Rightarrow\ \textbf{Big Rupture} \end{cases} \qquad\Longrightarrow\qquad \text{a new } U_x \text{ is formed}$$

  ```latex
  \begin{cases} U_x \text{ crosses the critical threshold at the boundary of } U_0 & \Rightarrow\ \textbf{Big Bang} \\[4pt] U_x \text{ crosses the critical threshold at the boundary of } U_+ & \Rightarrow\ \textbf{Big Rupture} \end{cases} \qquad\Longrightarrow\qquad \text{a new } U_x \text{ is formed}
  ```

  * $U_+$ must not be confused with c⁺: **c⁺ is a phase and lies inside the universe; $U_+$ is a limiting condition of the whole and lies outside it.** $U_+$ does not mean "a great deal of c⁺"; it is a global resolution condition.
  * The *Big Rip* of standard cosmology is an event that **ends** the universe. In Evertophy, the Big Rupture is an end and at the same time a new birth. A shared word does not show that the meanings are the same.
  * That a new $U_x$ is formed is canon; whether this takes place through the **same** production process as the birth at $U_0$ is open (Section 2.8).

* **Non-necessity / flexibility of the cycle:** The global circuit is not a mandatory, periodic time conveyor. It is not necessary for $U_x$ to reach one of the global thresholds, nor for the universe to come to an end: **the Dynamic Universe may or may not come to an end.** There may be a direct relation between $U_0$ and $U_+$; but it has no representation in any known sense. Where there is no representation, Evertophy passes no judgement (Principle of Silence, Section 2.8).

---

* **$C_m$ (Matter / Atom / Particle Phase):** The phase structure in which the physical reality we directly interact with exists: differentiated massive matter, atoms and subatomic particles, measurement processes, and the time we experience as a unidirectional flow. Physical time, three-dimensional Euclidean space, the metric and the speed-of-light limit are defined **only in Cₘ**.
  * **Kinematic membership band:** The kinematic band required for a physical structure to be a member of the Cₘ phase as matter is $0<v<c$. $v=c$ is not the band itself but its **liminal boundary**; light (the photon) lies on this boundary.

  $$\underbrace{0<v<c}_{C_m\ \text{matter membership}},\qquad \underbrace{v=c}_{\text{liminal boundary (light)}},\qquad \underbrace{c^+}_{\text{beyond-}c\text{ regime in which the band dissolves}}$$

  ```latex
  \underbrace{0<v<c}_{C_m\ \text{matter membership}},\qquad \underbrace{v=c}_{\text{liminal boundary (light)}},\qquad \underbrace{c^+}_{\text{beyond-}c\text{ regime in which the band dissolves}}
  ```

  * The **"0"** at the lower end of the band is a **relative-speed floor** defined with respect to any cosmological or inertial reference frame in the universe. A particle at rest in a local laboratory (even with laboratory speed $v_{\rm lab}=0$) is a member of the Cₘ phase because of its internal dynamics, spin structure and cosmological drift, and it remains inside the membership band. In other words, $v$ here is not a laboratory coordinate speed but a **membership reading**; the measured local speed does not by itself determine phase membership. This reading does not presuppose a classical "privileged frame"; the precise mathematical definition of $v$ is open (Section 3.3).

  $$v_{\rm lab}=0\;\not\Rightarrow\;c_0,\qquad 0<v<c\;\neq\;\text{laboratory coordinate speed}$$

  ```latex
  v_{\rm lab}=0\;\not\Rightarrow\;c_0,\qquad 0<v<c\;\neq\;\text{laboratory coordinate speed}
  ```

  * **Speed of light (c):** In the Evertophy ontology, the speed of light is the **regime threshold boundary** of the Cₘ phase. For the structure of matter, the speed of light is an unsurpassable universal constant; but it is not an unsurpassable speed limit for the Dynamic Universe as a whole. The speed of light is the boundary of the band within which the matter phase can exist; it is the resolution limit of time and of the Cₘ phase. The other side of this boundary is the **c⁺ phase regime**.
  * **Bose–Einstein condensate (BEC):** The BEC, in which atoms pass into a single macroscopic quantum state at temperatures close to absolute zero, is **the medium in the matter phase that is closest to the c₀ environment**. The BEC is not c₀: time flows inside it and the system remains in Cₘ. But its collective single-structure behaviour, in which the distinction between "this atom" and "that atom" loses its operational content, is the clearest example of the c₀ intuition attainable in the laboratory.
  * The experimental anchor points of this ontological approach are: quantum entanglement ("spooky action at a distance") and the material structure of the BEC.

---

* **$c_0$ (Potential / Black-Hole Phase):** The phase in which physically measurable matter loses its atomic and particle properties, and all particles merge — to a degree that converges to the metric-gapless state at the Planck scale — into the form of **a single integrated structure**. **In the Evertophy ontology, the black hole and the c₀ phase are ontologically identical** (author's ruling). In the black-hole phase, time and matter do not exist in the sense in which we can measure them: **they are undefined, but potentially present — not yet established.**
  * **Pre-temporal and pre-geometric regime:** c₀ is a pre-temporal and pre-geometric library of potential, in which three-dimensional Euclidean space, time and differential structure ($\nabla$, $\Box$) have not yet been established. In this phase, Cₘ-type length, duration and metric are undefined; mass, in turn, is tracked inside c₀ not as Cₘ-type rest mass but as a total magnitude and core capacity read from Cₘ (see below).
  * **Ontology of observation:** c₀ cannot be observed directly, because no observer can be inside it and it cannot be measured from within with the instruments of Cₘ. Its traces, however, are read in Cₘ in two ways:
    * **Cosmological and astrophysical traces:** The event horizons of black holes, gravitational time dilation, EHT shadow images and gravitational waves are measured phenomena. Evertophy reads them as indirect macroscopic traces of the c₀ structure on the Cₘ screen.
    * **Laboratory analogues:** The BEC, superfluidity and superconductivity in environments close to absolute zero are Cₘ environments in which the system passes into a single holistic quantum state. They are not c₀ itself but **the environments in the matter phase that are closest to the c₀ environment**; the similarity lies in collective single-structure behaviour (analogy, not identity).
  * **The black-hole core and the rejection of the "infinite singularity" reading:** The singularity that classical general relativity gives for the centre of a black hole — in the sense of "infinite density and zero volume" — is read in Evertophy as a **regime error**: a result that spills over the domain of validity of the theory. A black hole is neither an annihilation nor an infinite singularity (author's ruling).
    * **Metric-gapless single structure (maximal saturation):** The structure inside the event horizon is **contiguous**, finite and **of a single form**, to a degree that converges to the metric-gapless state at the Planck scale. This statement does not mean "the black-hole core is Planck-sized"; what it means is that the mass structure inside the black hole is **entirely of a single form**, in which almost no metric gap remains between particles. This is the core logic of Evertophy's reading of c₀ and of black holes. The saturation is written through a reading of $X$; $X$ itself is not a scalar, and what converges to saturation is its saturation reading:

    $$\xi_{\rm sat}=\pi_{\rm sat}(X)\;\longrightarrow\;\xi_{\rm sat}^{\max}\qquad(\text{in the normalized reading }\xi_{\rm sat}\approx1)$$

    ```latex
    \xi_{\rm sat}=\pi_{\rm sat}(X)\;\longrightarrow\;\xi_{\rm sat}^{\max}\qquad(\text{in the normalized reading }\xi_{\rm sat}\approx1)
    ```

    | Symbol | Meaning |
    |---|---|
    | $X$ | Dynamic meta-resolver; it is not a single number (Section 4) |
    | $\pi_{\rm sat}$ | The saturation readout projection of $X$ |
    | $\xi_{\rm sat}$ | Saturation reading; when normalized, its upper bound is 1 |

    * **Loss of individuality:** Matter that crosses the event-horizon threshold and touches the c₀ structure is not annihilated, nor is it lost inside an infinite singularity; it loses its identity and separate addressability as a proton, electron or atom and **integrates into a single collective structure**. A particle that touches its surface through the Force of Attraction integrates with this structure, regardless of whether it is entangled. The particle outside may or may not keep its spin; spin and entanglement are preserved as long as there is no interaction with the environment (measurement or contact with matter). Mass is conserved independently of spin (author's ruling).
    * **Mass, volume and horizon:** The counting unit of core capacity is the **Planck mass** (author's ruling). The density of the single structure cannot exceed the Planck density. In addition — as a separate author's ruling — a more massive black hole is **not denser**; it has a **larger volume** of the same metric-gapless structure, and therefore a wider event horizon. Exact proportionality between volume and mass holds in the constant-density realization ($\rho_{c_0}=\rho_*$), in which the mean density is independent of mass (Section 3.2). The ratios are as follows:

    $$N_{c_0}=\frac{M}{m_P},\qquad \rho_{c_0}\le\rho_P=\frac{c^5}{\hbar G^2}\approx5.16\times10^{96}\ \mathrm{kg/m^3},\qquad V_{c_0}\ge\frac{M}{\rho_P}=N_{c_0}\,\ell_P^3$$

    ```latex
    N_{c_0}=\frac{M}{m_P},\qquad \rho_{c_0}\le\rho_P=\frac{c^5}{\hbar G^2}\approx5.16\times10^{96}\ \mathrm{kg/m^3},\qquad V_{c_0}\ge\frac{M}{\rho_P}=N_{c_0}\,\ell_P^3
    ```

    $$R_{c_0}\propto M^{1/3}\ \ (\rho_{c_0}\ \text{constant}),\qquad R_s=\frac{2GM}{c^2}\propto M,\qquad A=4\pi R_s^2\propto M^2,\qquad \frac{R_{c_0}}{R_s}\propto M^{-2/3}$$

    ```latex
    R_{c_0}\propto M^{1/3}\ \ (\rho_{c_0}\ \text{constant}),\qquad R_s=\frac{2GM}{c^2}\propto M,\qquad A=4\pi R_s^2\propto M^2,\qquad \frac{R_{c_0}}{R_s}\propto M^{-2/3}
    ```

    | Symbol | Meaning |
    |---|---|
    | $N_{c_0}$ | Core capacity counted in units of the Planck mass |
    | $m_P=\sqrt{\hbar c/G}$ | Planck mass ($\approx2.18\times10^{-8}$ kg) |
    | $\rho_P$, $\ell_P$ | Planck density and Planck length; $\rho_P\,\ell_P^3=m_P$ |
    | $V_{c_0}$, $R_{c_0}$ | Effective Cₘ volume and radius of the single structure ($R\propto M^{1/3}$ in the constant-density realization, $\rho_{c_0}=\rho_*$; the smallest values with $\rho_*=\rho_P$) |
    | $R_s$, $A$ | Horizon (Schwarzschild) radius and horizon area |

    As mass increases, both the volume of the single structure and the horizon grow, but **not at the same rate**: the radius of the single structure grows with the cube root of the mass, the horizon radius with the mass, and the horizon area with the square of the mass. Taking the density at its Planck value ($\rho_*=\rho_P$; the smallest radius), the radius of the single structure for a black hole of one solar mass is about $4.5\times10^{-23}$ m: much larger than the Planck length ($1.6\times10^{-35}$ m) and much smaller than the horizon radius (about 3 km). For this reason the event horizon and the boundary of the single structure are not counted as the same thing; the internal profile of the single structure and its relation to the horizon are open (Section 3.2, APPENDIX K). The scaling of capacity with mass, and of horizon area with the square of mass, is consistent with the observationally tested area theorem; the area law, which states that entropy is proportional to area, is by contrast a theoretical result that has not yet been measured (Section 23.3).

  * **Time, space and change in c₀ — the pre-temporal regime** *(historical name: stasis lock)*: Evertophy's emergent-time equation is as follows (the normalized form of the core scaffold; the microphysical definition of $\mathcal{G}_{C_m}$ is open):

    $$dt = \Gamma\,\mathcal{G}_{C_m}\,d\lambda, \qquad \Gamma = \tau_0\,\tilde{\Gamma}$$

    ```latex
    dt = \Gamma\,\mathcal{G}_{C_m}\,d\lambda, \qquad \Gamma = \tau_0\,\tilde{\Gamma}
    ```

    | Symbol | Meaning |
    |---|---|
    | $\lambda$ | Dimensionless ordering parameter; it is not physical time |
    | $\mathcal G_{C_m}$ | The structure of distinguishable change in Cₘ; defined only in Cₘ |
    | $\tau_0$ | Time scale; it carries the dimension of time, $[\tau_0]=T$ |
    | $\tilde\Gamma$ | Dimensionless readout coefficient, $[\tilde\Gamma]=1$ |

    This form is the normalized realization (Section 7.3) of the core scaffold ($dt = \Gamma\,\mathcal{G}_{C_m}\,d\tau$, Section 7.2); the $\tau$ in the core form is likewise a formal ordering variable. $\mathcal{G}_{C_m}\,d\lambda$ is the amount of distinguishable change in Cₘ.
    * **Time = the reading of distinguishable change in Cₘ:** Time is the emergent reading of measurable change of state, and only in the Cₘ phase; change of position is one example of it. $\mathcal{G}_{C_m}$ is a Cₘ object: in c₀ it does not become zero, it is **not defined**.

      $$T(c_0) = \bot_0, \qquad \mathcal{G}_{C_m}\big|_{c_0} = \bot_0 \neq 0$$

      ```latex
      T(c_0) = \bot_0, \qquad \mathcal{G}_{C_m}\big|_{c_0} = \bot_0 \neq 0
      ```

    * **Time has not stopped; it has not yet been established:** Inside Cₘ, $\mathcal{G}_{C_m}=0$ means "there is a clock, but it does not advance"; this is a Cₘ state. c₀ is different: there, the very concept of a clock cannot be established. For this reason time in c₀ does not "flow slowly", nor is it "a frozen river"; it is undefined in the time language of Cₘ. Writing "$dt=0$" is only a pedagogical shorthand.

      $$\bot_0 \neq \bot_+ \neq 0$$

      ```latex
      \bot_0 \neq \bot_+ \neq 0
      ```

      | Symbol | Meaning |
      |---|---|
      | $\bot_0$ | The "not yet established" absence in c₀ |
      | $\bot_+$ | The "dissolved" absence in c⁺ |
      | $0$ | The real numerical zero — a Cₘ value |

    * **Discrete particles and Euclidean space are not established in c₀:** In c₀, differentiated Cₘ matter goes to the limit of a collective single structure. The distinction between "this particle" and "that particle", and the distance between them, lose their operational content. Cₘ-type three-dimensional Euclidean space and the metric are not destroyed in c₀; they are **not yet established** ($g_{\mu\nu}$: $\bot_0$). Dissolution in the sense of "no longer there" ($\bot_+$) belongs to c⁺.
    * **Heat, temperature and accessible energy:** Temperature is the statistical measure of the disordered (thermal) motion of particles in Cₘ; heat is the energy transferred in this way. Both require Cₘ time and discrete particle motion. For this reason, temperature, heat, free thermodynamic processes and the standard $dS/dt$ language are not defined in c₀ ($\bot_0$; Section 16.1). Energy, however, is not annihilated: the energy in c₀ is not read in Cₘ as accessible energy; total energy is conserved at the level of $U_x$ (Section 16.7a).
    * **c₀ is not nothingness:** What is absent is not c₀ itself, but the Cₘ concepts of time, space and temperature in c₀. c₀ is the potential/core phase in which time is not established; this is not a temporal priority but a position of regime (Section 3.2).

  * **The relation of the c₀ phase to absolute zero (0 Kelvin)** — **Author's ruling**: $c_0 \neq \text{Absolute Zero}$
    * **Conceptual distinction:** Absolute zero is the thermodynamic limit state within Cₘ itself; it is a Cₘ state. According to the third law of thermodynamics, it cannot be reached in a finite number of steps. And when it is approached, the system remains in Cₘ: quantum zero-point motion continues, clocks run and $dt$ is defined. The most precise atomic clocks in fact work with ultra-cold atoms. In c₀, by contrast, the very concept of temperature is undefined. In short, absolute zero is the coldest state of Cₘ; c₀ is not a state of Cₘ at all.

      $$T \to 0\,\mathrm{K} \;\Rightarrow\; C_m \text{ ground state}, \qquad T(c_0) = \bot_0$$

      ```latex
      T \to 0\,\mathrm{K} \;\Rightarrow\; C_m \text{ ground state}, \qquad T(c_0) = \bot_0
      ```

    * **BEC — the Cₘ environment closest to the c₀ environment, not c₀ itself:** In ultra-cold experiments that approach absolute zero (Bose–Einstein condensation), below the critical temperature a macroscopic fraction of the atoms fills the same quantum state. The system is described by a single macroscopic wave function (a "super-atom"); the distinction between "this atom" and "that atom" loses its operational content. For this reason the BEC is the environment in the matter phase that is closest to the c₀ environment, and the clearest example attainable in the laboratory for the c₀ intuition of a **collective single structure**; superfluidity belongs to the same family. The similarity lies only in collective single-structure behaviour: the BEC is not timeless; time flows inside it and the system remains in Cₘ. Cooling alone does not lead to c₀; what is approached in a BEC is not c₀ but the ground state of Cₘ.

      $$\text{BEC} \neq c_0 \qquad (\text{the environment in the matter phase closest to the } c_0 \text{ environment; analogy, not identity})$$

      ```latex
      \text{BEC} \neq c_0 \qquad (\text{the environment in the matter phase closest to the } c_0 \text{ environment; analogy, not identity})
      ```

    * **Black holes and the Hawking temperature:** The Hawking temperature attributed to a black hole is interpreted as the thermal reading that the horizon gives to a distant Cₘ observer; it is not the internal temperature of c₀. This is a candidate reading; the relation between the horizon and the phase boundary, and the quantitative bridge, are open (Section 3.2).

      $$T_H = \frac{\hbar c^3}{8\pi G M k_B}$$

      ```latex
      T_H = \frac{\hbar c^3}{8\pi G M k_B}
      ```

In summary, **c₀ (the Core Phase)** is the **pre-temporal core regime** in which the measurable change belonging to Cₘ, three-dimensional Euclidean geometry and time are **not yet established**; in which matter is integrated into a single structural form that converges to the metric-gapless state; and in which information is potentially preserved in this integrated structure without being destroyed. Time in c₀ has not stopped; it has not yet been established.

---

* **$c^+$ (Dark Matter / Dark Energy / Vacuum Phase — Dissolved Phase):** All of the structure of the universe that is not in the form of matter (the entire particle structure) or of black holes is the c⁺ phase: cosmic voids, the vacuum medium, dark matter and dark energy — and **even the space between the particles that make up the atom** is c⁺ (author's ruling). In the Evertophy ontology, c⁺ (the Timeless Upper Phase / Archive / Coordination Layer) is the upper coordination and archive layer of the complete-triangle phase architecture ($c_0 \leftrightarrow C_m \leftrightarrow c^+ \leftrightarrow c_0$), in which the constraints of massive matter, Euclidean geometry and the flow of time dissolve; historically, it was described as possessing "informational texture integrity" (graph state / texture integrity).

  $$\boxed{\;\text{Every region read as "empty space" on the }C_m\text{ screen — from intergalactic voids to the space between the particles inside the atom — is the reading of }c^+\text{ in }C_m\text{.}\;}$$

  ```latex
  \boxed{\;\text{Every region read as "empty space" on the }C_m\text{ screen — from intergalactic voids to the space between the particles inside the atom — is the reading of }c^+\text{ in }C_m\text{.}\;}
  ```

  Since three-dimensional space is dissolved in c⁺, it is not correct to say "c⁺ is located somewhere"; the correct statement is that what Cₘ reads as "empty space" is c⁺. This ruling does not change the measured results of atomic physics; the predictions of quantum mechanics inside the atom remain fully valid as the computational language of Cₘ.

  * **Ontological status — dissolution of Cₘ matter support and the timeless archive:**
    * **Matter-support dissolution:** In the c⁺ phase, the massive particles, atomic structures and three-dimensional Euclidean metric ($g_{\mu\nu}$) specific to the matter phase we live in (Cₘ) are not valid:

      $$\mathrm{supp}_{C_m}(\text{matter}) \to \varnothing, \qquad g_{\mu\nu}^{(C_m)} \to \bot_+$$

      ```latex
      \mathrm{supp}_{C_m}(\text{matter}) \to \varnothing, \qquad g_{\mu\nu}^{(C_m)} \to \bot_+
      ```

      In the c⁺ phase, material mass, Euclidean length or physical duration cannot be preserved as such; these magnitudes belong to the readout instruments of the Cₘ screen.
    * **Timeless archive (provenance registry):** c⁺ is not a metaphysical source of the future. It is the universal library of memory in which all the sealed records (sealed provenance) realized in the Cₘ phase are present together as a whole, without requiring temporal ordering. Being present together does not mean being accessible from Cₘ; access depends on an explicit readout law (Section 15.2).
  * **Canonical identity:** In the Evertophy ontology, the c⁺ phase is the phase identity of the dark sector, of the vacuum medium and of the empty spaces inside the structure of matter:

    $$\boxed{\;c^+\;\equiv\;\text{Dark Matter (DM)}\;\equiv\;\text{Dark Energy (DE)}\;\equiv\;\text{Vacuum}\;}$$

    ```latex
    \boxed{\;c^+\;\equiv\;\text{Dark Matter (DM)}\;\equiv\;\text{Dark Energy (DE)}\;\equiv\;\text{Vacuum}\;}
    ```

    The "vacuum state" of quantum field theory, and the calculations made with it, are **the computational language within Cₘ** of this ontological identity; every effect measured in the vacuum is accepted unconditionally and is interpreted in Evertophy as the reading of c⁺ in Cₘ.

    Dark matter and dark energy, which astrophysicists search for as two separate mysterious structures or particles, are not two different entities in the Evertophy ontology. Both are different observational projections (readout modes) of the same c⁺ phase, which appear according to the environment in which it is found when seen from the Cₘ screen:

    $$
    c^+ \text{ Phase}
    \longrightarrow
    \begin{cases}
    \text{At galactic / cluster scale (clustered)}
    &\longrightarrow \text{Dark Matter (DM) reading} \\[4pt]
    \text{At cosmological scale / in voids (sustaining expansion)}
    &\longrightarrow \text{Dark Energy (DE) reading} \\[4pt]
    \text{At laboratory scale}
    &\longrightarrow \text{Vacuum reading}
    \end{cases}
    $$

    ```latex
    c^+ \text{ Phase}
    \longrightarrow
    \begin{cases}
    \text{At galactic / cluster scale (clustered)} &\longrightarrow \text{Dark Matter (DM) reading} \\[4pt]
    \text{At cosmological scale / in voids (sustaining expansion)} &\longrightarrow \text{Dark Energy (DE) reading} \\[4pt]
    \text{At laboratory scale} &\longrightarrow \text{Vacuum reading}
    \end{cases}
    ```

    Identity does not mean that the observed densities are equal; the ontological identity and the quantitative mechanism required for each observational phenomenon are separate things (Section 11.2).

  * **Two appearances according to location and scale** *(also found in the archive under the names "bound" and "free")*:
    * **In galaxy clusters — clustered c⁺ (the dark-matter reading):**
      * **Candidate reading:** c⁺ clusters together with galaxies and with dense clusters of Cₘ baryonic matter, under the constraint coherence of the Force of Attraction.
      * **Observational reading:** To the Cₘ observer, this clustering appears as an **additional gravitational effect** that does not radiate and cannot be detected as a particle, but holds galaxy rotation curves (RAR) together and produces gravitational lensing. The source is c⁺; the language of the reading is the gravitational and metric language of Cₘ; this reading does not add Cₘ-type rest mass or a metric to c⁺. The quantitative mechanism is open.
    * **In cosmic voids and at the horizon of the universe — expansion-sustaining c⁺ (the dark-energy reading):**
      * **Candidate reading:** Because matter density is very low in the great cosmic voids between galaxies, the pressure of matter on c⁺ and the sealing constraint weaken.
      * **Observational reading:** c⁺ that moves away from matter returns to its own natural relaxation regime. This is read not as an external "repulsive force" or an "anti-gravity fluid" but as a **volumetric phase expansion** arising from the release of the constraint. The Cₘ observer measures this expansion as the accelerating expansion of the universe (dark energy). The measured acceleration is accepted unconditionally; its connection with c⁺ is an Evertophy reading, and its quantitative mechanism is open.

---

  * **Emergent time, sealing and the record architecture:**
    * **The dissolution of time in c⁺:** In Evertophy, physical time is not an independently running clock; it is an emergent reading that depends only on distinguishable change in the Cₘ phase ($\mathcal{G}_{C_m}$). In c⁺ there is no time, and no constraint that depends on time. This is a different absence from the "not yet established" absence in c₀: in c⁺, the time that had been established in Cₘ is **dissolved**. Writing "$dt_{c^+}=0$" is, as in c₀, only a pedagogical shorthand; the ontological statement is $\bot_+$, and $\bot_+$ is not the numerical zero.

      $$T(c^+)=\bot_+,\qquad \mathcal G_{C_m}\big|_{c^+}=\bot_+\neq0,\qquad dt_{c^+}=0\ \ (\text{pedagogical shorthand only})$$

      ```latex
      T(c^+)=\bot_+,\qquad \mathcal G_{C_m}\big|_{c^+}=\bot_+\neq0,\qquad dt_{c^+}=0\ \ (\text{pedagogical shorthand only})
      ```

    * **Sealing and the arrow of time:** Time exists only in Cₘ and is unidirectional; otherwise causality would disappear, because in the matter phase everything stands in a cause–effect relation. Phase transitions are bidirectional; but the record channel is **unidirectional** (Cₘ → c⁺), and once sealing has taken place the record is fixed (author's ruling). The unidirectional flow of time is associated with the fact that the record produced in Cₘ is written into c⁺ in this way — **append-only** and irreversibly. The "registry" is not an independent entity; it is the readout language used for this trace (Section 7.7a). As the computational language within Cₘ for the process at the moment of measurement, completely positive and trace-preserving quantum channels (CPTP, Kraus representation) can be used; these tools are not the definition of sealing, but a Cₘ model (Section 15.2).

      $$\text{gate: } c_i\rightleftarrows c_j\ \ (\text{bidirectional}),\qquad \text{record channel: } C_m\longrightarrow c^+\ \ (\text{unidirectional; seal fixed})$$

      ```latex
      \text{gate: } c_i\rightleftarrows c_j\ \ (\text{bidirectional}),\qquad \text{record channel: } C_m\longrightarrow c^+\ \ (\text{unidirectional; seal fixed})
      ```

    * **Sealing cost $C_s$ — candidate reading:** Sealing a change from Cₘ into the c⁺ archive, and reading a c⁺-sourced effect on the Cₘ screen, may incur a processing cost. Reading this cost in Cₘ as gravitational time dilation and write latency — that is, as the relativity of time — is a **candidate reading**; it renames the measured results of general relativity and does not count as deriving them (Sections 8.7, 14.3).

---

  * **Human perception of time, the $0<v<c$ band and the c⁺ regime:**
    * **Perception and anomalies:** Human perception and measuring instruments measure entirely with the outputs of the Cₘ phase. Because we look at measurement results from inside Cₘ, we assume the unidirectional flow of time and the speed-of-light limit to be absolute. For this reason we classify the effects that appear at the boundary between c⁺ and Cₘ — quantum entanglement, tunnelling, the dark sector — as "impossible anomalies" or as "spooky faster-than-light effects".
    * **The beyond-light-speed regime:** In the Cₘ phase, material existence is possible only in the $0<v<c$ band. The c⁺ phase is the beyond-$c$ upper regime in which this band dissolves. There is no "faster-than-light particle motion" there, because in c⁺ the language of metric distance, time and particle acceleration is not defined. That c⁺ is beyond $c$ does not mean a Cₘ-type motion with $dx/dt>c$ (Section 3.4).
    * **Structures on the boundary:** A structure can lie on the boundary of two regimes. A structure on the $v=c$ boundary of Cₘ is at the same time on the boundary of the c⁺ regime: the two regimes share this boundary. Lying on the boundary is not the same as being a member of either phase; light and gravitational waves propagate on this shared boundary, and the quantum and subatomic level also displays the behaviours of this boundary (Section 18.0).

      $$\text{boundary}(C_m)\big|_{v=c}\;=\;\text{boundary}(c^+)\big|_{C_m\text{ side}},\qquad \text{liminal}\neq C_m\ \text{membership},\qquad \text{liminal}\neq c^+\ \text{membership}$$

      ```latex
      \text{boundary}(C_m)\big|_{v=c}\;=\;\text{boundary}(c^+)\big|_{C_m\text{ side}},\qquad \text{liminal}\neq C_m\ \text{membership},\qquad \text{liminal}\neq c^+\ \text{membership}
      ```

---

  * **Quantum phenomena and boundary regimes:**
    * **The Hartman effect — a timeless phase shortcut (compatible reading):** In quantum tunnelling, the fact that the traversal time of a particle inside the barrier does not grow with barrier thickness but saturates at a constant value (the Hartman effect) is a measured phenomenon. Evertophy reads this not as the particle crossing the barrier step by step in three-dimensional Euclidean space, but as the record meeting the barrier being temporarily loosened (unsealing), the particle using the metric-free c⁺ phase shortcut, and being sealed again behind the barrier (resealing). In this reading no Cₘ time proportional to thickness is produced inside the barrier; the saturation of the traversal time is consistent with this.
    * **Quantum entanglement:** Two particles that appear far apart in Cₘ are connected through a single timeless relation in the c⁺ layer (historically called the operation group / graph state). Entanglement is not a signal that travels faster than light in Cₘ; it is the timeless relation in the c⁺ layer becoming visible through measurement in Cₘ (phase projection). c⁺ is not a communication channel: this reading does not make signalling possible.
    * **The double-slit experiment (candidate reading):** As long as the particle is not measured, it displays the non-local, wave-like behaviour of the boundary between c⁺ and Cₘ; at the moment a measurement is made, it is recorded and sealed on the Cₘ screen as a particle.
    * **Neutrinos — structures on the phase-regime boundary:** Neutrinos, whose existence is known but which are hard to measure, are read as the structures most weakly bound to the Cₘ phase, closest to the boundary between mass and light. Their change of flavour during travel (neutrino oscillation) is a robustly measured phenomenon and is a candidate for this boundary reading. The 2011 measurement claiming that neutrinos travel faster than light was withdrawn as a measurement error; Evertophy does not rely on that result.
    * **Energy from the vacuum and pair production:** Under the identity c⁺ ≡ vacuum, the vacuum calculations of quantum field theory are the computational language of c⁺ in Cₘ. The production of particles from the vacuum by a strong electric field (the Schwinger mechanism), or pair production from high-energy photons, is read as a local phase transition triggered by external energy on the c⁺ → Cₘ edge (candidate reading).

---

  * **Experiments and observations read as compatible with the c⁺ phase:**

In Evertophy, the c⁺ phase is treated not as an abstract hypothesis but as a reading that is in contact with the traces of experiment and observation. None of the following examples is evidence for c⁺; each is a measured phenomenon together with an Evertophy reading that is **compatible** with it. The measurements are accepted unconditionally; the readings are optional.

1. **The Stuttgart quantum teleportation experiment (November 2025, Nature Communications):** The polarization state was transferred, with about 72% success, between photons emitted by two independent quantum dots. The quantum teleportation protocol requires a Bell measurement and **a classical communication channel**; the transfer of information is not completed without this classical channel. The Evertophy reading does not change this measured structure: c⁺ is read as **the source of the correlation** that makes the transfer possible; it is not a signal-carrying channel. Photons are structures on the $v=c$ boundary, that is, on the shared boundary of Cₘ and c⁺.
2. **Hartman-effect tunnelling measurements:** The saturation of the tunnelling time at a value independent of barrier thickness is a reading compatible with the timeless c⁺ shortcut.
3. **The Casimir effect:** The measured force between two conducting plates in the vacuum is interpreted, under the identity c⁺ ≡ vacuum, as the reading of c⁺ on Cₘ; the standard quantum-field-theory calculation of the force remains fully valid as the computational language of Cₘ.
4. **Lene Hau and the BEC experiments:** The extreme slowing and stopping of light inside a Bose–Einstein condensate is read as light from the $v=c$ boundary being slowed inside the environment in the matter phase closest to the c₀ environment; the merging of atoms into a single quantum wave is the laboratory analogue of the c₀ intuition of a collective single structure.
5. **The delayed-choice quantum eraser:** Standard quantum mechanics explains this experiment without any backward-in-time effect. The results are **compatible** with the reading that c⁺ is an archive that does not require temporal ordering; this reading involves no retrocausation or signalling, and it is not a "certification" of the experiment.
6. **The ANITA anomaly:** The ultra-high-energy events recorded by the balloon experiment over Antarctica, which appear to come upward through the Earth, are still debated. Evertophy is a candidate for reading them as a cosmic-scale tunnelling leak (candidate reading).

### Properties of the c⁺ phase (a reading compared with the matter phase)

| Property / Magnitude | $C_m$ (Matter Phase) | $c^+$ (Timeless Upper Phase / Archive) |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Kinematic membership** | $0<v<c$ (matter); $v=c$ liminal boundary | Beyond-$c$ regime; Cₘ matter support is dissolved |
| **Physical time** | Present and emergent ($dt_{C_m}>0$) | **Dissolved** ($\bot_+$; pedagogical shorthand $dt_{c^+}=0$) |
| **Metric tensor ($g_{\mu\nu}$)** | Defined (3D Euclidean base space; effective 3+1 reading) | **Undefined / metric-free** ($\bot_+$) |
| **Ontological identity** | The phase in which measurement and indirect effects are read | **$c^+\equiv\text{DM}\equiv\text{DE}\equiv\text{Vacuum}$** |
| **Galaxy-cluster reading** | Stars, gas and visible mass | **Clustered c⁺ — the dark-matter reading** |
| **Cosmic-void reading** | Thinning of matter | **Expansion-sustaining c⁺ — the dark-energy reading** |
| **Laboratory reading** | Measurement apparatus | **Vacuum** |
| **Inside the atom** | Particles (nucleus, electrons) | **The space between particles** |
| **Quantum manifestation** | Measured particle / event | **Source of the entanglement correlation** (candidate reading) |

---

* **$c$ (Speed of Light):**
  * **Consistency constraint (phase threshold):** In standard physics, the speed of light is regarded as an unsurpassable universal speed constant. In Evertophy, the speed of light is **the record-resolution limit and phase threshold of the Cₘ phase**. In Cₘ, the speed of light is a definitional point interval, $c_{C_m}=[c,c]$.
  * **Change of regime (phase gate):** A massive body inside Cₘ cannot reach the speed of light by accelerating. At this boundary, "matter moving faster than light" does not come into being; what is at stake is not an acceleration but **a phase transition into the c⁺ regime**: what is crossed is not a speed value but the membership band of Cₘ. After the transition, the massive form of matter dissolves and the speed language of Cₘ is undefined:

    $$\boxed{\;\mathrm{supp}_{C_m}(\text{matter})\to\varnothing\;}$$

    ```latex
    \boxed{\;\mathrm{supp}_{C_m}(\text{matter})\to\varnothing\;}
    ```

  * **Liminal state (the photon):** Light itself (the photon) lies on the $v=c$ boundary. It is neither a member of the massive matter of Cₘ nor inside c⁺; it lies on **the shared boundary** of the two regimes: the boundary of the Cₘ band is at the same time the boundary of the c⁺ regime. Gravitational waves also propagate on the same boundary (author's ruling); it is known from measurement that gravitational waves propagate at the same speed as light.

### Consequences of the three-phase structure

In short: on one side of **Cₘ**, the differentiated matter phase we live in and measure, stands the pre-temporal potential phase **c₀**; on the other side stands the atemporal upper phase **c⁺**. These "side" words are conceptual, not spatial: the phases are connected not by a chain but by a complete triangle. Together these three phases form the Dynamic Universe, $U_x$. Outside this universe there are two limiting conditions: the absolute source $U_0$ and absolute dissolution $U_+$.

The most important consequence of this structure is this: fundamental concepts such as time, three-dimensional Euclidean space and the speed-of-light limit are valid **only in Cₘ**. In c₀ these structures are not yet established; in c⁺ they are dissolved. In other words, "time" or "space" are not things that exist in the same sense everywhere in the universe; they are structures specific to one phase.

This perspective opens a place for pieces that seem disconnected. The direction of time can be associated with the unidirectionality of the record written into c⁺. Measurement is positioned as an event belonging to Cₘ. Dark matter and dark energy are read as two appearances of the same c⁺ phase: c⁺ clustered with matter appears as dark matter, expansion-sustaining c⁺ as dark energy; and in the laboratory c⁺ appears to us as the vacuum. Entropy is the dissolution of Cₘ structure into c⁺; energy is not annihilated, it is sealed in c⁺. The most fundamental principle of this structure is the **Force of Attraction**: the four known forces — gravitation included — are its different projections according to scale, amount of mass and phase, and the three phases stand together beneath it in a balance. Which regime the phases resolve into is determined by the dynamic meta-resolver $X(x)$: the structure that resolves which phase regime is valid in a given situation and which states are admissible.

To be honest, none of these readings has yet been proven. Evertophy's canonical numerical-prediction counter still stands at **0/10**. This document sets out the ontological skeleton of the framework: what is what, what is related to what, and what is forbidden. It does not offer numerical proof. In terms of its scientific standing, Evertophy is a TOE research programme containing a three-phase, two-level ontology, selected formal results and conditional effective models; a definite physical selection principle and a distinctive, preregistered prediction are still open.

$$\boxed{\;\text{Canonical numerical-prediction counter: } \mathbf{0/10}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{Canonical numerical-prediction counter: } \mathbf{0/10}\;}
```

### How this search was tested: the cross-AI marathon

However coherent a framework may appear, it may contain errors that escape the eye of its builder. For this reason, between **23 and 26 September 2026**, we put Evertophy through as severe an audit as possible.

Seven AI systems — Claude, ChatGPT Math&Physics, ChatGPT Thought Experiments, WolframGPT, Kimi, Qwen and Grok — tested Evertophy's equations, types and ontological claims over 20 rounds, auditing one another's outputs. The results were then compiled over four rounds and passed once more through cross-audit. Throughout the marathon, Evertophy's own rules were deliberately suspended; every AI was free to criticize and to propose derivations. Only two rules were recalled in every round: the Range Formalism, and the principle that the fundamental structures are valid only in Cₘ.

The aim was not to produce new predictions. The aim was to test the **internal consistency** of the framework with the highest confidence, to weed out faulty claims, to seal the correct ones formally, and to mark honestly what remains open. The results can be gathered under three headings.

**What was verified.** The type and algebraic skeleton of the framework was formally encoded with the theorem prover Lean: 269 theorem and lemma declarations — 46 in the isolated production core and 223 in the archive — none with an unfinished proof. Two different kinds of "absence" — the not-yet-establishedness in c₀ and the dissolvedness in c⁺ — were separated from each other; in the production core, constructing a value in the wrong phase was blocked at the type level for the value families specific to Cₘ. The complete-triangle gate structure defined by the author was formally encoded, and it was proven within this encoded system that quantities specific to Cₘ — time, space, metric, internal speed, the speed-of-light limit and rest mass — **cannot be transported through any gate**. Lean did not derive the triangle from nature; it checked the consequences of the structure given by the author. Moreover, this lock does not prevent c⁺-sourced effects from being read in Cₘ. It was also proven that the archive algebra reaches a single record however the same sequence of events is grouped; the order of the events, by contrast, is always preserved.

**What was weeded out.** More than thirty claims were refuted by counterexamples or withdrawn with their scope narrowed: an old quantum threshold that contained a dimensional error, an asymmetry criterion that turned out to distinguish nothing, a fit threshold that could have rejected a correct prediction, and others like them. Some of these came from the framework's own older texts, some from the proposals of the AI systems taking part in the marathon. The retired claims were not deleted; they stay on the record together with the reasons why they were refuted.

**What remains open.** Perhaps the most important result was to locate one gap definitively. It was shown — formally at the finite level, and by analytic counterexamples at the continuous level — that the bridge from $X$ to measurable physical quantities **cannot be uniquely selected** by type rules and well-definedness conditions. In other words, the framework's point of contact with observation requires a physical selection principle and observation. This debt was not hidden; it was recorded as a frozen open debt.

The most important thing the marathon taught me was this: the truth of a claim is determined not by how many agree with it, but by evidence of the appropriate kind. Seven AI systems reaching the same conclusion is not a proof. And when there is no evidence, leaving a question open is always better than forcing it closed.

### What this document offers

This document is the text prepared towards the first publishable version of the Evertophy Core Ontology. The results of the marathon, and my own later direct clarifications, have been worked into the relevant sections; each concept is given a single, current account. The version history and the audit trail are kept outside this document, in the V24 rolling master. Parts that are not yet closed or that have alternatives have not been hidden; they are marked explicitly. The open debts and pending decisions are in APPENDIX K.

There is one more thing I must say clearly: the consensus of the cross-AI marathon is not, by itself, canon. In Evertophy, the final word on what is canon belongs to the author.

*— Uğur Yılmaz*

*This section was written by the author in his own narrative voice; Claude made only corrections of spelling, notation and alignment with the current canon. The English version was translated from the Turkish by Claude.*

## How to read this document

**What this document does:** It explains **what Evertophy essentially is**: which entities it speaks of, how they are related to one another, and what each concept expresses. The reader can follow it without consulting any other document.

**What this document does not do:** It does not offer a derivation, does not produce numerical predictions and does not prove theorems. Where formal results and models are mentioned, their scope and status are written next to them.

**Style of presentation:** Internal development jargon (version labels, status stamps) is avoided as far as possible. The confidence with which a statement is made is indicated directly in the sentence: **canon**, **author's ruling**, **model**, **bridge**, **candidate reading**, **compatible reading**, **open** or **retired**. In every part that contains an equation, the equation comes first, immediately followed by copyable LaTeX code and then by an explanation of the symbols. The function of the equation is then summarized in a short five-question table:

| Question | What the answer explains |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | The function of the equation or structure |
| **Why does it do it?** | Which gap, error or need it addresses |
| **How does it do it?** | With which method, which assumption and which tool |
| **What result is expected?** | What it is intended to provide once established |
| **What result is obtained?** | Its present state and status |

## Note on source discipline

This monograph is not a summary of a single archive; it is a distilled form of many layers. The direct working base is the previous current core text (minor12). Archive surveys, self-correction reports and decision records were parsed item by item without bulk transfer; only missing scope compatible with the current core was added. A fact found in a source and a tool's interpretation of it are kept separate.

Cross-AI proposals do not carry equal authority. A proposal that contradicts the current direct ontology is excluded from the core. A useful but underived proposal is set aside as a **model**, a **bridge** or a **formal candidate**; old terminology as **historical**; and an undecided meaning as an **open debt**. The coverage of the archive survey is high; but since there is no file manifest and no hash-based completeness check, no claim of a "cryptographically complete survey" is made.

This document is therefore not a compilation that pours the whole archive onto a single plane, but **an integrated core to which version precedence has been applied**. In case of conflict, the valid order of precedence is:

$$\boxed{\;\text{Direct author clarification} \;>\; \text{current core} \;>\; \text{current formal canon} \;>\; \text{compatible formal candidate} \;>\; \text{historical archive} \;>\; \text{tool interpretation}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{Direct author clarification} \;>\; \text{current core} \;>\; \text{current formal canon} \;>\; \text{compatible formal candidate} \;>\; \text{historical archive} \;>\; \text{tool interpretation}\;}
```

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It determines which of several conflicting sources is valid |
| **Why does it do it?** | The framework developed over months with many tools and versions; a statement that appears "settled" in an old record may conflict with a newer author's ruling |
| **How does it do it?** | It ranks sources by authority: the author's direct clarification stands at the top, a tool's interpretation at the bottom |
| **What result is expected?** | The historical richness is preserved without being mixed into the current core |
| **What result is obtained?** | If an old statement conflicts with a newer direct clarification, it does not enter the current core; it remains in the archive as a historical record |

The version history, correction records and audit trail are kept in the current V24 rolling master, published alongside this text. This text was compiled under the author's direction, with cross-AI auditing: compiler Claude; auditors ChatGPT (Math&Physics, Thought Experiments, Codex), WolframGPT, Kimi, Qwen and Grok.




# SECTION 0 — ENTRY GATE

## 0.1 Evertophy in one paragraph

Evertophy is a framework which proposes that reality is not a medium of a single kind but a structure in which **three distinct phases** coexist. Like the ice, liquid and vapour states of water; but this time the phases are not of matter but of **reality itself**. These three phases are called **c₀** (the pre-temporal potential / core phase), **Cₘ** (the phase of differentiated matter, measurement and time; the phase we live in) and **c⁺** (the atemporal upper / archive / coordination phase). The central proposal of the framework is this: physical laws are not rules that exist arbitrarily *within* these phases, but forms that arise from the structure of relations and transitions *between* the phases. A single higher object organizes this structure: the **dynamic meta-resolver** $X(x)$. Before all of these stands an unnumbered premise: the **Force of Attraction** (Turkish: *Çekim Kuvveti*), the fundamental ontological postulate of Evertophy.

## 0.2 Evertophy on one page

Modern physics is divided into four great domains: general relativity, quantum mechanics, the Standard Model and cosmology. Each of the four is extraordinarily successful on its own, but they do not fully understand one another's language. Evertophy's starting question is this: *if these four domains describe the same reality, what is their common grammar?*

The proposed answer is not a unification equation but a **resolver**. This object, called $X(x)$, resolves phase, context and admissibility. The selection of actual physical coefficients, of the constitutive map or of the observable value belongs to a separate layer: to the physical selector, the bridge and the readout law.

Six basic consequences follow from this picture:

**First, time is not fundamental.** In Evertophy, physical time exists only in Cₘ and arises from the measurable change there. In c₀ time is not yet established; in c⁺ the time established in Cₘ is dissolved.

**Second, physical quantities are interval-valued.** A quantity is written in the form $[P^-, P^+]$. This does **not** mean "every constant varies": a range of zero width ($[P,P]$) is a fully valid, static and deterministic case.

**Third, the Force of Attraction is the fundamental ontological postulate.** The four known forces — gravitation included — are its projections according to scale, amount of mass and phase; the Force of Attraction itself belongs to the structure.

**Fourth, the metric is defined only in Cₘ.** $g_{\mu\nu}$ is not an admissible object in c₀ and c⁺; but in the two phases **for different reasons**: in c₀ it is not yet established, in c⁺ it is dissolved.

**Fifth, irreversibility lives not in the phase gate but in the record.** The transitions themselves are bidirectional; what cannot be undone is the **stamp** imprinted during the transition.

**Sixth, the framework has two levels.** The Dynamic Universe ($U_x$) formed by the three phases is a whole, and this whole has two limiting conditions: the global saturation pole $U_0$ and the global dissolution pole $U_+$. The phases are inside, the poles outside; the two do not belong to the same ontological class.

### The heart of the framework

$$\boxed{\;\text{In Evertophy's fundamental working mechanism, the tendencies of matter and texture are read together with the language of inter-phase tension and transformation.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{In Evertophy's fundamental working mechanism, the tendencies of matter and texture are read together with the language of inter-phase tension and transformation.}\;}
```

This entry record, approved by the author, requires that the Force of Attraction be read neither as merely a Newtonian force nor as merely a passive Cₘ geometry. The **clustering tendency** on the matter side and the **expansion tendency** on the texture / c⁺ side produce a continuous relation and transformation among c₀, Cₘ and c⁺; this is the main narrative axis of the framework.

This narrative **does not replace Postulate 0**: the ontological priority of the Force of Attraction is not regarded as derived from entropy. The expressions "matter entropy" and "texture entropy" are used here as a phase-specific language of tendency; they do not mean that standard thermodynamic entropy is carried over unchanged into c₀ and c⁺. For this reason the correct term is **the fundamental working mechanism of the universe**; the language of an "engine" is not used.

*Notation note:* In this document, expressions such as "inside / outside" or "on one side / on the other side" are notations at the ontological and type level; they are not a spatial ordering. At the global level there is no metric and no space; and the phase topology is not a chain but a complete triangle.

## 0.3 What Evertophy does *not* claim

Understanding a framework also requires knowing what it does not claim:

- **It does not claim to be a completed Theory of Everything.** It is a research programme that aims at a Theory of Everything (0.6): a conceptual order and a mathematical skeleton.
- **It does not claim to derive the constants of the Standard Model.** There are assignments of mechanisms; none of them produces a number.
- **It does not claim to solve the cosmological constant problem.**
- **It does not claim to solve the black-hole information paradox.**
- **It does not claim to explain entanglement;** it proposes a reading framework.
- **It does not forbid unification; it forbids the multiplication of after-the-fact explanations.** The same mechanism may explain more than one independent phenomenon; but the results must follow from the same, previously defined chain of derivation, without additional tuning, and with separate falsification conditions for each observable.
- **It carries no active claim of empirical evidence.**

## 0.4 Explicit declaration of the empirical status

$$\boxed{\;\text{Canonical numerical-prediction counter: } \mathbf{0/10}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{Canonical numerical-prediction counter: } \mathbf{0/10}\;}
```

The multi-domain correlation line, once regarded as the framework's strongest empirical claim, **has been permanently closed by internal audit** (Section 21). The empirical assets that remain today are **constraints placed on** Evertophy; they are not predictions that follow from Evertophy.

### The 0/10 → 1/10 gate

For an item to move the counter, it must satisfy six conditions together:

| # | Condition |
|---|---|
| **i** | It must be **derived** from the ontology |
| **ii** | Its parameters and bridge assumptions must be **fixed in advance** |
| **iii** | It must be **distinguishable** from what the standard approach gives |
| **iv** | The prediction interval must be **preregistered before the target data are seen** |
| **v** | The **data lineage must be sealed**, including the data version, the target selection and the held-out test set |
| **vi** | It must have been tested on **blind data** |

$$\boxed{\;\text{No item counts as } 1/10 \text{ unless all six conditions are satisfied together.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{No item counts as } 1/10 \text{ unless all six conditions are satisfied together.}\;}
```

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It defines the minimum conditions required for a claim to count as a "prediction" |
| **Why does it do it?** | Retrospective fitting, tuning while looking at the data and definitional identities can look like predictions; the correlation line closed in the past is an example |
| **How does it do it?** | It combines the six conditions into a single gate; if even one is missing, the item does not count |
| **What result is expected?** | That the counter increases only with predictions that are genuinely derived, distinguishable and blind-tested |
| **What result is obtained?** | Today no item satisfies all six conditions together; the counter stands at **0/10** |

## 0.4a The distinction between ontology and model

In this document, **ontology** denotes the entities, types and structural relations that the framework takes as fundamental; **model** denotes the mathematical realization of this ontology in a particular physical regime.

$$\boxed{\;\text{MODEL}\neq\text{ONTOLOGY}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{MODEL}\neq\text{ONTOLOGY}\;}
```

The observational success of a model does not by itself show that all the mathematical objects it uses are fundamental ontological entities. For example, the metric and the language of general relativity within Cₘ may be indispensable for physical readout; but this does not permit the metric to be carried into c₀ or c⁺ as a fundamental ontological object.

Evertophy unconditionally accepts established experimental and observational results as an **external physical standard of comparison**; it does not equate the acceptance of its own postulates, ontological interpretations and bridge mechanisms with these external results. The task of the framework is not to ignore existing physics, but to propose an explicitly marked, additional ontological and interpretive structure.

**The scope of unconditional acceptance.** What is accepted unconditionally is the **measurement result** verified by the measurement community with its own methods — together with its uncertainty, its record of systematic errors and its calibration status. Results corrected or withdrawn by the measurement community itself (for example, the 2011 OPERA superluminal-neutrino measurement) are accepted together with that correction. What is unconditional is methodological honesty: no ontological reading may alter a reliable observational result in its own favour; if a measurement contradicts an Evertophy reading, it is the reading that changes.

## 0.6 Scope and present status of the research programme

Evertophy is an ontology and physics research programme that aims at a Theory of Everything (TOE). This text presents the three-phase, two-level basic definitions, selected formal results, conditional model alternatives and open physical bridges, together with their scope. The TOE aim indicates the research direction of the programme; it does not confer the status of a completed or experimentally verified TOE. The canonical numerical-prediction counter stands at **0/10**.

## 0.7 Note on parallel literature

The lines of literature that the framework has intersected during its development are on record. The following families are related-work comparisons based on bibliographic records published alongside this text. The common theme is parallelism; it is not derivation or verification.

| Line | Relation |
|---|---|
| Verlinde (entropic gravity) [Ver11] | Reads gravitation not as a fundamental force but as an entropic and emergent phenomenon; this is a different thesis from Evertophy's fundamental postulate of the Force of Attraction. The parallel lies only in the intuition that attraction is not reduced to particle exchange |
| Jacobson (from thermodynamics to general relativity) [Jac95] | The thermodynamic origin of general relativity — methodological parallel |
| Holographic principle [tH93, Sus95] | The boundary–bulk relation — structural parallel |
| AdS/CFT [Mal98] | Correspondence between a boundary theory and a bulk gravitational theory — structural parallel |
| Bekenstein–Hawking entropy [Bek73, Haw75] | The relation $S_{BH}\sim A$; held in the framework as a theoretical result not yet tested by observation (23.3) |
| Analogue gravity (Barceló–Liberati–Visser) [BLV05] | Acoustic metric and transformation optics — the most realistic relative as a publication gateway |
| Volovik (the superfluid He-3 universe) [Vol03] | A phase-based reading of reality — among the candidates for the closest intellectual relative |
| The black-hole cosmology family [Pat72, Dym92, Pop10, FMM89, FMM90] | Independent similar intuitions. The distinctive parts of Evertophy (the critical threshold at the boundary of $U_0$, the joint birth of the three phases in the same constitution, the resolver $X$) showed no direct match in the comparison set examined; no comprehensive survey of priority and originality has been carried out |

# SECTION 1 — BASIC INTUITION AND POSTULATES

## 1.1 Where the framework came from

The conceptual beginning of Evertophy goes back to 20 July 2025. The first question came not from physics but from **architecture and systems thinking**: *why does the same structure look different at different scales of a system?*

Three first intuitions were born from this question and were put on record:

- The centre of a black hole may not be a singularity but **a finite structure integrated into a single form**.
- That the same essence looks different at different scales requires a **hierarchy of regimes**.
- Dark matter and dark energy may be **two appearances of the same thing**.

This origin determines the character of the framework: Evertophy was born not from an equation but from a **search for structural coherence**.

## 1.2 Postulate 0 — the Force of Attraction

$$\boxed{\;\text{The Force of Attraction is the most fundamental ontological postulate of Evertophy.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{The Force of Attraction is the most fundamental ontological postulate of Evertophy.}\;}
```

The four known forces — gravitation, the strong force, the weak force and electromagnetism — are **different projections of the Force of Attraction according to scale, amount of mass and phase**:

$$\boxed{\;\{\text{Gravitation},\ \text{Strong},\ \text{Weak},\ \text{Electromagnetism}\} \;=\; \Pi_{\text{scale, mass, phase}}\big[\,\text{Force of Attraction}\,\big]\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\{\text{Gravitation},\ \text{Strong},\ \text{Weak},\ \text{Electromagnetism}\} \;=\; \Pi_{\text{scale, mass, phase}}\big[\,\text{Force of Attraction}\,\big]\;}
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $\Pi_{\text{scale, mass, phase}}$ | Projection operation: the form the Force of Attraction takes at a given scale, amount of mass and phase; **its formal definition is open (APPENDIX K.1)**. The box is a statement of a postulate, not a mathematical unification equation: the four interactions are mathematical objects of different kinds, and the output type of $\Pi$ has not yet been defined |
| Force of Attraction | Fundamental ontological postulate; phase-independent constraint coherence |

**The Force of Attraction is not merely a phenomenon of "pulling".** At the ontological level, the Force of Attraction is the **phase-independent constraint coherence** that is necessary for the structures permitted in all of the phases c₀, Cₘ and c⁺: the fundamental bond that keeps the three phases consistent with one another. Constraint coherence does not require a metric; for this reason it is also valid in c₀ and c⁺, where there is no metric. In Cₘ this principle becomes visible through its projections: gravitation is its **metric projection**, and general relativity is the effective language of that projection.

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It positions the four forces as different appearances of a single fundamental principle |
| **Why does it do it?** | The common origin of the four forces is one of the open questions of modern physics; Evertophy ties this origin to an ontological postulate |
| **How does it do it?** | It defines the Force of Attraction as phase-independent constraint coherence, and reads each force as a projection ($\Pi$) dependent on scale, mass and phase |
| **What result is expected?** | Once the formal definition of the projection operation is established, the measured properties of the four forces can be read from a single source |
| **What result is obtained?** | The postulate is established at the ontological level; the formal definition of $\Pi$ and the detailed projection account of the forces are open |

### Concept lock

$$\boxed{\;\text{Force of Attraction (Çekim Kuvveti)} \;\neq\; \text{Gravitation (Kütleçekim)}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{Force of Attraction (Çekim Kuvveti)} \;\neq\; \text{Gravitation (Kütleçekim)}\;}
```

"Force of Attraction" (Turkish: *Çekim Kuvveti*) is the canonical name; its ontological nature is not a classical force field but phase-independent constraint coherence. In no context is the Force of Attraction to be written as "gravitation", "gravity" or "gravitational force" (Turkish: *kütleçekim*, *kütle çekim kuvveti*). The statement "mass attraction is the fundamental axiom" in the early texts has been replaced by this definition: the fundamental postulate is the Force of Attraction; gravitation is one of its projections.

### Five distinct objects — not to be confused

$$\boxed{\;\text{Force of Attraction}_{\rm ontoloji} \;\neq\; \text{Gravitation}_{C_m} \;\neq\; X \;\neq\; g_{\mu\nu} \;\neq\; G_{\rm eff}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{Force of Attraction}_{\rm ontoloji} \;\neq\; \text{Gravitation}_{C_m} \;\neq\; X \;\neq\; g_{\mu\nu} \;\neq\; G_{\rm eff}\;}
```

| Object | What it is |
|---|---|
| **Force of Attraction (ontology)** | Fundamental postulate; phase-independent constraint coherence |
| **Gravitation (Cₘ)** | The metric projection of the Force of Attraction in Cₘ; the subject of general relativity |
| **$X(x)$** | Dynamic meta-resolver; resolves the phase regime and the admissible states |
| **$g_{\mu\nu}$** | A geometric object belonging to Cₘ (the metric) |
| **$G_{\rm eff}$** | The effective gravitational coupling measured in Cₘ; model level |

These five are not the same thing and cannot be used in place of one another. In particular, **$X$ is not the Force of Attraction**; $X$ is the higher object that determines in which regime a physical structure is resolved.

### The coexistence of the three phases

That c₀, Cₘ and c⁺ form a single structure in $U_x$ is not a coincidence: the three phases stand together **in a balance** under the constraint coherence of the Force of Attraction. The bidirectionality of the phase gates is the expression of this balance: Cₘ structure dissolves into c⁺, and c⁺-sourced effects are read in Cₘ. The formal equation of the balance is open. The balance explains why the phases **stand together**; in order to explain **why there are exactly three** phases, it would have to show that it does not permit any other number of phases. The number of phases is an open question (Section 24).
