### Present status of the projections

| Projection | Status |
|---|---|
| Gravitation | The metric projection in Cₘ (Section 8) |
| Electromagnetism | Draft reading: charge is a persistent signature related to the phase boundary $c$. The projection form of repulsion and of charge — **To Be Addressed Later** |
| Strong force | Historical draft reading (under the old name "crystal phase") — **To Be Addressed Later** |
| Weak force | Draft reading: transformation from an unstable to a stable structure — **To Be Addressed Later** |

Measurements show that the four forces do not only attract: in electromagnetism, like charges repel each other; the weak force is related to particles changing their kind; in the electromagnetic and strong forces, what is decisive is not mass but charge. Since the Force of Attraction is ontologically a principle of coherence, it is conceptually possible for its projections in Cₘ to appear in the form of attraction, repulsion or transformation; the detailed account will be taken up later. It has been measured that the strengths of the forces change with energy scale, and that the electromagnetic and weak forces unify at high energy. This picture is consistent with the idea of "projection according to scale"; but it is not evidence.

## 1.3 Four postulates

### P1 — Plurality of phases

Reality is not a single homogeneous medium. There are at least three distinguishable phase regimes, and relation and transition between them are possible.

**Explanation.** This does not reject the assumption that "the same physics is valid everywhere"; it states that the valid laws are **the appearances of the same laws in different regimes**.

### P2 — Law arises from relation

Physical laws are not internal properties of the phases; they are the expression of the structure of relation and transition between phases.

**Example.** That the speed of light is an upper limit is not an arbitrary rule in Evertophy: it is **the consistency constraint** of the Cₘ phase; it sets the limit of information processing, of record and of resolution.

### P3 — A single resolver

There is a single higher object that determines the phase regime: $X(x)$. It would be possible to assume three separate regulators for three phases; the framework **rejects** this.

### P4 — The Range Formalism

$$\boxed{\;\text{Physical numerical syntax is INTERVAL-VALUED: } P \in [P^-,\, P^+]\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{Physical numerical syntax is INTERVAL-VALUED: } P \in [P^-,\, P^+]\;}
```

And the critical subtlety — this is not a prohibition:

$$\boxed{\;[P,\,P] \text{ is a FULLY VALID static, deterministic physical range.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;[P,\,P] \text{ is a FULLY VALID static, deterministic physical range.}\;}
```

$$\boxed{\;\text{Being interval-valued} \;\nRightarrow\; \text{being time-variable.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{Being interval-valued} \;\nRightarrow\; \text{being time-variable.}\;}
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $P$ | Any physical quantity |
| $P^-$, $P^+$ | The lower and upper bounds of the quantity |
| $[P,P]$ | A range of zero width: an exact, constant value |
| $\nRightarrow$ | "Does not imply" |

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It specifies how physical quantities are to be written: as ranges |
| **Why does it do it?** | Measurements and model parameters actually come as bands; a single point value hides uncertainty |
| **How does it do it?** | It carries every quantity in the form $[P^-,P^+]$; an exact constant is written as the zero-width $[P,P]$ |
| **What result is expected?** | That prediction bands and observation bands can be compared honestly (Section 6) |
| **What result is obtained?** | The rule is established; no special threshold mechanism or structural justification is sought for a zero-width range. P4 is a rule of syntax; it does not say "everything varies" |

## 1.4 Two core ideas

### Idea 1 — The same language, different licences

The four great domains describe the same reality, but they use different mathematical licences. Their common grammar is the language of the phase structure.

### Idea 2 — Measurement is specific to Cₘ

Measurement is not a separate fourth ontological phase; **the domain in which direct physical measurement can be made is Cₘ**. The "observer", too, is not a separate ontological class, but the role of systems inside Cₘ that produce physical records and readouts.

Consequently, everything said experimentally about c₀ and c⁺ must be tested **through a reading in Cₘ**.

## 1.5 Classes of entities

| Class | What it is | Example |
|---|---|---|
| **Ontological entity** | Structure belonging to reality itself | c₀, Cₘ, c⁺, $X$, the Force of Attraction |
| **Global node** | A global ontology layer that is not a phase | $U_0$, $U_x$, $U_+$ — not phases |
| **Formal object** | Mathematical representation | $g_{\mu\nu}$, the stamp monoid, a range |
| **Readout** | What is actually measured in Cₘ | $\alpha$, $G$, $H_0$, mass |

Mistaking a quantity of the readout layer for an ontological entity is the most common error in the framework.

## 1.6 Hygiene of definitions — symbols that must not be confused

| Symbol | What it is | Not to be confused with |
|---|---|---|
| $U_0$ | Absolute source / pre-layer | $u_0$, $u(0)$ — these are historical process coordinates |
| $X(x)$ | Dynamic meta-resolver | $X(u)$ — the historical logistic kernel |
| $C_m$ | Phase name | $c_m$ — a table component |
| $x$ | General condition variable | Not a particular coordinate; a **wildcard** |
| $c$ | Speed of light / Cₘ consistency constraint | The phase names $c_0$, $c^+$ |

These distinctions are not bureaucratic: two meanings of the same letter have led to real errors in formal work.

## 1.7 Additional notational safety

Two notational distinctions are especially preserved:

| Notation | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $C_m$ | **Phase name**: the matter / measurement / emergent-time phase |
| $c_m$ | If used for a table, coefficient or subcomponent, it is only a local symbol; it is not a phase name |
| $X(x)$ | The current dynamic meta-resolver |
| $X(u)$ | Historical / operational scalar kernel; not identical with the current $X(x)$ |
| $X_A, X_z, X_t$ | If used, these are only **domain or readout projections** or historical names; they are not three separate fundamental $X$'s |

This document accepts **a single $X$** in the fundamental ontology. Domain labels may be used only as projection notation:

$$X_A \equiv \Pi_A[X],\qquad X_z\equiv\Pi_z[X],\qquad X_t\equiv\Pi_t[X]$$

```latex
X_A \equiv \Pi_A[X],\qquad X_z\equiv\Pi_z[X],\qquad X_t\equiv\Pi_t[X]
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $\Pi_A$, $\Pi_z$, $\Pi_t$ | Projections of $X$ onto the atomic/nuclear, redshift and time domains |
| $X_A$, $X_z$, $X_t$ | Names of these projections; they are not separate ontological regulators |

These notations do not create separate ontological regulators, and they do not bring the retired old quantile / correlation line back into the canon.

To avoid confusion over extreme values, the safe phase-regime notation is:

$$\boxed{\;X_{\rm state}\in I_0,\qquad X_{\rm state}\in I_m,\qquad X_{\rm state}\in I_+\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;X_{\rm state}\in I_0,\qquad X_{\rm state}\in I_m,\qquad X_{\rm state}\in I_+\;}
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $X_{\rm state}$ | The resolved-state face of $X$ |
| $I_0$, $I_m$, $I_+$ | Classes of admissible regimes / states for the corresponding phase; not necessarily numerical intervals |

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It writes safely the projection of the single $X$ onto domains and its membership in phase regimes |
| **Why does it do it?** | The scalar extreme-value language "$X\to0$ / $X\to1$" and the impression of three separate $X$'s in the old texts contradicted the non-scalar nature of $X$ |
| **How does it do it?** | It defines domain labels as projections ($\Pi$) and phase regimes as state classes ($I$) |
| **What result is expected?** | That every scalar operation performed on $X$ is carried out through an explicitly defined reading |
| **What result is obtained?** | The notation is established; the old scalar extreme-value language is not carried into the current $X(x)$ ontology |



# SECTION 2 — $U_0$: THE ABSOLUTE SOURCE / PRE-LAYER

## 2.1 Current definition

$$\boxed{\;U_0 \;\rightsquigarrow\; \{c_0,\; C_m,\; c^+\}_{\text{together, in the same constitution}}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;U_0 \;\rightsquigarrow\; \{c_0,\; C_m,\; c^+\}_{\text{together, in the same constitution}}\;}
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $U_0$ | Absolute Core: the pre-layer, the absolute generative source |
| $\rightsquigarrow$ | "Permits / gives birth to": not the first link of a causal chain, but the source of the condition of existence |
| $\{c_0, C_m, c^+\}$ | The three phases |
| "together, in the same constitution" | The structural joint birth (co-genesis) of the three phases; it does not mean a common external clock |

**$U_0$** is the **pre-layer**, that is, the absolute generative source, which at the Big Bang forms the three phases **together, in the same constitution**. By the author's ruling: in $U_0$, all the structures that make up the Dynamic Universe — c₀, Cₘ and c⁺ — exist **in a single structural form**, contiguous to a degree that converges to the metric-gapless state at the Planck scale. This is not an assignment of a numerical mass or size (2.10); it describes the unity of the three phases before their birth.

**Joint birth (co-genesis):** c₀, Cₘ and c⁺ emerge together in the $U_0 \to U_x$ constitution. The author's expression "simultaneous" describes this structural joint birth. There is no common physical clock outside the phases, because physical time is defined only in Cₘ. $U_0$ does not produce one first and then the other; all three are born at once. This is not a sequential production but **a structural birth**.

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It defines the source of the three phases and the manner of their birth |
| **Why does it do it?** | Readings in which the phases are derived from one another (for example, c⁺ being born from Cₘ) break the foundation of the ontology |
| **How does it do it?** | It writes the three phases as structures born together, in the same constitution, from a single source; it reads the arrow not as causal but as a condition of existence |
| **What result is expected?** | That no phase is modelled as a derivative of another; that time is not mistaken for a clock outside the Big Bang |
| **What result is obtained?** | Joint birth is canon; the full characterization of $U_0$ is a reserved domain set aside for the author |

## 2.2 What $U_0$ is not

$$\boxed{\;U_0 \notin \mathrm{Obj}(\mathcal{C}_{\rm faz})\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;U_0 \notin \mathrm{Obj}(\mathcal{C}_{\rm faz})\;}
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $\mathcal{C}_{\rm faz}$ | The phase category: the structure whose objects are c₀, Cₘ, c⁺ and whose arrows are the phase gates |
| $\mathrm{Obj}(\cdot)$ | The objects of a category |
| $\notin$ | "Is not a member of" |

$U_0$:

- **is not a phase**; it is not a fourth sector,
- **is not a gate endpoint**; there is no **ordinary phase gate** leading to or from it (the global relation of birth and limit, 2.8, is separate from this),
- **is not a subtype of c₀**,
- **is not a topological hole**,
- **is not a transition fibre**,
- **is not consciousness, a god, a mind** or a metaphysical entity,
- **is not an observable object**.

**Why these limits?** $U_0$ is the element that stands on the epistemically weakest ground in the framework. Turning it into a dynamic sector or a geometric structure would move the most fragile point of the theory to its centre.

## 2.3 Historical readings

During the development of the framework, different readings of $U_0$ were tried. They are **historical lineage**, not the current definition:

| Historical reading | Why it is not current |
|---|---|
| A physical universe core carrying a numerical mass value | It reduces $U_0$ to a Cₘ quantity |
| The universe-scale counterpart or threshold of c₀ | It reduces $U_0$ to a phase |
| An upper bound on addressing / capacity | It is a metaphor, not an ontological definition |
| A cosmogonic dissolution threshold | It is a threshold, not a source |
| $u(0) \equiv U_0$ | $u$ is a historical process coordinate; it is not $U_0$ |

These readings are preserved in the archive, but none of them enters the current core.

$$\boxed{\;u(0) \;\neq\; U_0\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;u(0) \;\neq\; U_0\;}
```

## 2.4 $U_0$ and the Big Bang

The current core preserves two separate but compatible layers together:

1. **The rule of birth:** At the beginning of the Big Bang, $U_0$ forms the three phases together, in the same constitution.
2. **The double-limit principle:** The two limits that would form the basis of a cyclic operation are preserved in the Core; the full mechanism of these limits is set aside for V24.

### Joint birth — the ontological origin

$$\boxed{\;U_0 \rightsquigarrow \{c_0,\;C_m,\;c^+\}_{\text{together, in the same constitution}}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;U_0 \rightsquigarrow \{c_0,\;C_m,\;c^+\}_{\text{together, in the same constitution}}\;}
```

This statement is not a sequential phase transition. Since $U_0$ is not an object of the phase category (2.2), an ordinary phase-transition arrow of the form $c_0\to U_0$ or $c^+\to U_0$ **is not set up**.

**Author's ruling — the phases are not derived.** c₀ and c⁺ are formed at the Big Bang together with Cₘ; they are not derived from Cₘ. What is modelled is the Cₘ → c⁺ transition and the sealing of time; for this purpose the vacuum, the BEC and subatomic structures are considered.

### What the Big Bang is not

| It is not | Why |
|---|---|
| **A classical explosion** | It is not a picture of a centre and outward spreading |
| **Creation from nothing** | $U_0$ exists |
| **Motion in a pre-existing space** | Space and time are born with Cₘ |
| **Time beginning like a primordial substance** | Time is not a substance; it is produced in Cₘ |

$$\boxed{\;\text{TIME IS NOT AN EXTERNAL CLOCK ABOVE the Big Bang.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{TIME IS NOT AN EXTERNAL CLOCK ABOVE the Big Bang.}\;}
```

The question "what was there before the Big Bang?" is categorically problematic from the point of view of Cₘ physical time, because the word "before" already presupposes a physical time.

### The double-limit principle — the Core / V24 cycle seed

Two limits are preserved in the framework as a **Core seed**:

- **the c₀ saturation / collapse limit,**
- **the dilution / closed-cycle limit.**

These two limits do not by themselves carry all the mechanical arrows of the old narratives into the canon. The safe current statement is:

$$\boxed{\;\text{limit condition}\;\Longrightarrow\;\text{a cycle seed that could be tied to a new }U_0\text{-mediated birth condition}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{limit condition}\;\Longrightarrow\;\text{a cycle seed that could be tied to a new }U_0\text{-mediated birth condition}\;}
```

The $\Longrightarrow$ here is not a **phase gate**; it denotes the cycle condition at the meta level. $U_0$ is not inserted into the phase graph and is not defined as a "physical object that is formed again". Nor does the dilution branch assume a time flowing inside c⁺; this limit is understood only as an asymptotic cycle condition from the point of view of a reading made from Cₘ.

$$\boxed{\;\text{joint birth}\;\neq\;\text{double-limit cycle mechanism}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{joint birth}\;\neq\;\text{double-limit cycle mechanism}\;}
```

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It preserves the two extreme limits of the universe (collapse and dilution) as the seed of a cyclic operation |
| **Why does it do it?** | The two extreme tendencies in Evertophy's three-phase architecture carry a natural possibility of a cycle at the global level |
| **How does it do it?** | It writes the limits not as phase gates but as a cycle condition at the meta level; it separates birth from mechanism |
| **What result is expected?** | That the cycle equations can later be established in the V24 layer |
| **What result is obtained?** | The seed is preserved in the Core; the cycle equations, the cycle index and the full link between $U_0$ and the limit condition are not defined in this version |

## 2.5 Two ontological levels — the phase level and the global universe level

Most of what has been described so far belonged to **a single level**: the three phases and the pre-layer that gives birth to them. But the full picture of the framework has two levels.

$$\boxed{\;\text{Evertophy consists of TWO ONTOLOGICAL LEVELS: the PHASE LEVEL and the GLOBAL UNIVERSE LEVEL.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{Evertophy consists of TWO ONTOLOGICAL LEVELS: the PHASE LEVEL and the GLOBAL UNIVERSE LEVEL.}\;}
```

| Level | Objects | Type of relation |
|---|---|---|
| **Phase level** | c₀, Cₘ, c⁺ | Complete bidirectional triangle; gates and stamps |
| **Global universe level** | $U_0$, $U_x$, $U_+$ | Global cycle; relations of birth and limit |

$$\boxed{\;U_0,\; U_x,\; U_+ \;\notin\; \{c_0,\, C_m,\, c^+\}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;U_0,\; U_x,\; U_+ \;\notin\; \{c_0,\, C_m,\, c^+\}\;}
```

**Why is this distinction necessary?** When the double-limit idea (2.4) is forced into a single level, it produces constant tension: an arrow such as "c₀ saturation → $U_0$" pulls an entity that lies **outside** the phase graph **into** the phase graph. Once the two levels are separated, the tension disappears by itself: the phases stay inside, the global poles stand outside.

### The ice–water–vapour analogy

Ice, water and vapour can exist together in a container. These are **regimes**: the phase level. The container as a whole is a separate object: the global level. If the whole system goes to an extreme condition, this is not a change of a single regime, but **the system itself** reaching an extreme state.

$$\boxed{\;U_0 \text{ and } U_+ \text{ are not RIVALS of the phases — they are the two LIMITING CONDITIONS of the global cycle.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;U_0 \text{ and } U_+ \text{ are not RIVALS of the phases — they are the two LIMITING CONDITIONS of the global cycle.}\;}
```

### Local phase limit ≠ global universe limit

A region approaching the c₀ regime (the formation of a black hole) is a **local** event, and it happens in the universe every day. Global saturation, by contrast, is a condition belonging to **the universe as a whole**. Even if the two are written in the same language, they are not the same thing.

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It separates the ontology into two levels: the phases and the global poles |
| **Why does it do it?** | Pulling the global poles into the phase graph produced false identities such as "c₀ saturation = $U_0$" |
| **How does it do it?** | It defines the global nodes outside the set of phases; it calls the relation between phases a gate and the global relation a cycle |
| **What result is expected?** | That local events (black-hole formation) are not confused with global conditions (the extreme states of the universe) |
| **What result is obtained?** | The two levels are canon and are also formally typed (equation set 5.3) |

## 2.6 $U_+$ — Absolute Dissolution / Absolute Rupture

$$\boxed{\;U_+ = \text{the limiting state in which the entire phase-structural resolution of the universe thins out until it crosses the global resolution limit}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;U_+ = \text{the limiting state in which the entire phase-structural resolution of the universe thins out until it crosses the global resolution limit}\;}
```

If $U_0$ is the global **saturation** pole, $U_+$ is the global **dissolution** pole.

**Author's ruling — equivalence of names.** "Absolute Dissolution" and "Absolute Rupture" describe the same limiting state: the first emphasizes the state itself, the second the whole $U_x$ structure reaching that state. The event that takes place when the whole of $U_x$ crosses the critical threshold at the boundary of $U_+$ is called the **Big Rupture** (2.8).

### What $U_+$ is not

| It is not | Why |
|---|---|
| **A phase** | It lies outside the phase graph |
| **A fifth node** | It is not added to the phase triangle |
| **c⁺** | c⁺ is a phase and lies inside $U_x$; $U_+$ is a pole outside |
| **$X$** | $X$ resolves the phase regime; $U_+$ is a global limit |
| **The future** | There is no Cₘ time at the global level either |
| **"The last stop / death"** | In every case a new $U_x$ is formed; it cannot be read in the manner of an apocalyptic narrative |
| **Merely "a great deal of c⁺"** | It is not a quantitative abundance of c⁺ but a global resolution condition |

**The highest risk of confusion is between $U_+$ and c⁺;** both are on the side of dissolution. The distinction in one sentence is:

$$c^+ = \text{phase (inside)} \qquad\qquad U_+ = \text{global pole (outside)}$$

```latex
c^+ = \text{phase (inside)} \qquad\qquad U_+ = \text{global pole (outside)}
```

### A pole is not a threshold

$U_0$ and $U_+$ are not **thresholds**; the threshold lies at the **boundary** where the extreme phase touches the global pole. The historical reading "$U_0$ = the threshold of c₀" (2.3) must not be brought back by this route.

### The function sentence for $U_+$ — open

$U_0$ has a function sentence: *it gives birth to the three phases together, in the same constitution.* The counterpart for $U_+$ has not yet been written. The following generalized form is a candidate, **not canon**:

$$U_\ast \;\rightsquigarrow\; \{c_0,\; C_m,\; c^+\}_{\text{together, in the same constitution}}, \qquad \ast \in \{0,\, +\}$$

```latex
U_\ast \;\rightsquigarrow\; \{c_0,\; C_m,\; c^+\}_{\text{together, in the same constitution}}, \qquad \ast \in \{0,\, +\}
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $U_\ast$ | One of the two global poles: $U_0$ or $U_+$ |

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It defines the global dissolution pole and separates it from the c⁺ phase |
| **Why does it do it?** | Since both are on the side of "dissolution", this is the pair most often confused |
| **How does it do it?** | It defines $U_+$ as a limiting state of the whole and c⁺ as a phase inside the universe; it records that the two names describe the same state |
| **What result is expected?** | That a function sentence can be established for $U_+$ as for $U_0$ |
| **What result is obtained?** | The definition and the equivalence of names are established; the birth function of $U_+$ is open, the generalized form is a candidate |

## 2.7 $U_x$ — the Dynamic Universe

$$\boxed{\;U_x = \text{the dynamic universal structure in which the three phases are realized together}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;U_x = \text{the dynamic universal structure in which the three phases are realized together}\;}
```

$U_x$ is not merely a drawing frame; it is the middle object of the global level. Time, space and measurement live inside this whole, in the Cₘ phase.

### Notational hygiene — three similar symbols

| Symbol | What it is |
|---|---|
| $U_x$ | The Dynamic Universe — an object of the global level |
| $U(x)$ | Historical or functional usage; it is matched with $U_x$ **only if the context clearly points to $U_x$** |
| $X(x)$ | **The dynamic meta-resolver** — an entirely different object (Section 4) |

$$\boxed{\;U_x \;\neq\; X(x) \qquad U_x \;\neq\; U_0 \qquad U_x \;\neq\; c_0,\, C_m,\, c^+\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;U_x \;\neq\; X(x) \qquad U_x \;\neq\; U_0 \qquad U_x \;\neq\; c_0,\, C_m,\, c^+\;}
```

Writing $U(x)\equiv U_x$ is not automatic: the current canonical notation is $U_x$. The ontological type of $U_x$ — "set membership" or "ontological inclusion" — **is an open question**.

### Equation set — the global level

$$U_0,\ U_x,\ U_+ \notin \{c_0,\ C_m,\ c^+\} \qquad \text{GlobalRel} \neq \text{Gate}$$

```latex
U_0,\ U_x,\ U_+ \notin \{c_0,\ C_m,\ c^+\} \qquad \text{GlobalRel} \neq \text{Gate}
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $U_0$ | Absolute Core — the critical threshold **at its boundary**: the **Big Bang** |
| $U_x$ | Dynamic Universe — **not a pole** |
| $U_+$ | Absolute Dissolution / Absolute Rupture — the critical threshold **at its boundary**: the **Big Rupture** |
| GlobalRel | The relation between global nodes |
| Gate | The gate between phases |

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It formally separates the phase level from the global level |
| **Why does it do it?** | Mistaking global nodes for phases collapses the two-level architecture |
| **How does it do it?** | It defines the global nodes outside the set of phases; it writes the global relation as a type of relation different from a gate |
| **What result is expected?** | That no model or code uses the global nodes as if they were phases |
| **What result is obtained?** | The distinction phase ≠ global node and the ruling "$U_x$ is not a pole" have been formally verified; the structural type of $U_x$ is open |

## 2.8 The global cycle and the two birth events

**Joint birth.** In the $U_0 \to U_x$ birth, c₀, Cₘ and c⁺ are formed **together, in the same constitution**. Since there is no common clock running outside the phases, this is not "the same moment" of a clock but co-presence in the same constitution. The beyond-$c$ character of c⁺ is not a property acquired later; it is a constitutive property of the phase. From birth onwards, the three phases stand together under the constraint coherence of the Force of Attraction (1.2).

$$\boxed{\;U_0 \;\leftrightarrow\; U_x \;\leftrightarrow\; U_+\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;U_0 \;\leftrightarrow\; U_x \;\leftrightarrow\; U_+\;}
```

When the extreme phase-regime limit is crossed:

$$\begin{cases} U_x \text{ crosses the critical threshold at the boundary of } U_0 & \Rightarrow\; \textbf{Big Bang} \\[4pt] U_x \text{ crosses the critical threshold at the boundary of } U_+ & \Rightarrow\; \textbf{Big Rupture} \end{cases} \qquad\Longrightarrow\qquad \text{a new } U_x \text{ is formed}$$

```latex
\begin{cases} U_x \text{ crosses the critical threshold at the boundary of } U_0 & \Rightarrow\; \textbf{Big Bang} \\[4pt] U_x \text{ crosses the critical threshold at the boundary of } U_+ & \Rightarrow\; \textbf{Big Rupture} \end{cases} \qquad\Longrightarrow\qquad \text{a new } U_x \text{ is formed}
```

### Five rulings

| # | Ruling |
|---|---|
| **G-1** | The Dynamic Universe **may come to an end** through absolute collapse or absolute dissolution — **or it may not** |
| **G-2** | Collapse end → critical threshold at the boundary of $U_0$ → **Big Bang** |
| **G-3** | Dilution end → critical threshold at the boundary of $U_+$ → **Big Rupture** |
| **G-4** | **In both cases** a new $U_x$ is formed |
| **G-5** | There may be a direct $U_0 \leftrightarrow U_+$ relation, but it **has no representation** |

### The Principle of Silence

$$\boxed{\;\textbf{Where there is no representation, Evertophy passes no judgement.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\textbf{Where there is no representation, Evertophy passes no judgement.}\;}
```

There may be a direct transition between $U_0$ and $U_+$; but it has no representation in any known sense, and what lies beyond belongs to metaphysics. Evertophy stays on the line of science, observation and experiment; here it **prefers to remain silent**. This is the same principle as c⁺ being a closed cycle (Section 11): *first the academic boundary, then the absence of observation.* The only difference is the layer: there at the phase level, here at the outermost layer.

### Four directions and their meanings

$$U_0 \Rightarrow U_x, \qquad U_x \Rightarrow U_0, \qquad U_x \Rightarrow U_+, \qquad U_+ \Rightarrow U_x$$

```latex
U_0 \Rightarrow U_x, \qquad U_x \Rightarrow U_0, \qquad U_x \Rightarrow U_+, \qquad U_+ \Rightarrow U_x
```

| Direction | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $U_0 \Rightarrow U_x$ | **Birth** — the formation of the three phases together, in the same constitution |
| $U_x \Rightarrow U_0$ | **Limit** — the collapse end touching the global pole |
| $U_x \Rightarrow U_+$ | **Limit** — the dissolution end touching the global pole |
| $U_+ \Rightarrow U_x$ | **Result of re-formation** — canonical; the production mechanism is open |

### Separating result from mechanism

G-4 could be read as two separate propositions; but they are **not the same claim**:

$$\boxed{\;U_+ \Rightarrow U_x' \qquad \textbf{CANONICAL: the RESULT of global re-formation}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;U_+ \Rightarrow U_x' \qquad \textbf{CANONICAL: the RESULT of global re-formation}\;}
```

$$\boxed{\;U_+ \rightsquigarrow \{c_0, C_m, c^+\}_{\text{together, in the same constitution}} \qquad \textbf{OPEN: the MECHANISM of birth}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;U_+ \rightsquigarrow \{c_0, C_m, c^+\}_{\text{together, in the same constitution}} \qquad \textbf{OPEN: the MECHANISM of birth}\;}
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $U_x'$ | The newly formed Dynamic Universe |

That a new $U_x$ is formed **is canon**; that this takes place through **the same** ontological production process as the birth at $U_0$ **is not canon**. The second proposition says that $U_+$ has the same birth process as $U_0$, and it is stronger; it remains open until $U_+$'s own function sentence is written (2.6).

### Four mandatory safeguards

**(i) The arrows connect to the whole, not to a phase.**

$$U_0 \leftrightarrow U_x \;(\text{relation with the whole}) \qquad \text{not} \qquad U_0 \leftrightarrow c_0 \;(\text{gate})$$

```latex
U_0 \leftrightarrow U_x \;(\text{relation with the whole}) \qquad \text{not} \qquad U_0 \leftrightarrow c_0 \;(\text{gate})
```

In this way the rule "$U_0$ is not the endpoint of any gate" is preserved. This is why the curly brackets are drawn in the core figure.

**(ii) Global bidirectionality is not mathematical invertibility.** The symbol $\leftrightarrow$ here shows the two ends of a relation; it **does not claim** the existence of an inverse operation.

**(iii) The cycle is not a conveyor belt.** Reading from left to right gives the impression of a process; but time is **inside** $U_x$. The global cycle is not a flow but a relation of **limiting conditions**.

$$\boxed{\;\text{Cycle index} \;\neq\; t\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{Cycle index} \;\neq\; t\;}
```

There is no clock between $U_x$'s. The order is **a logical provenance order**, like the order of records in the archive.

**(iv) The cycle is not a mandatory periodic circuit.** By G-1, the universe **does not have to** come to an end. The word "cycle" describes a possibility of closure, not a law of recurrence. Nor do these relations **enter** the topological computation of the phase triangle (Section 19).

### Symmetry — a measured statement

The collapse branch and the dissolution branch carry **a structural symmetry at the level of the global limit poles**. But the following correspondence **is not set up**:

$$U_0 : c_0 \;::\; U_+ : c^+ \qquad \text{(there is NO ontological isomorphism)}$$

```latex
U_0 : c_0 \;::\; U_+ : c^+ \qquad \text{(there is NO ontological isomorphism)}
```

This is because $U_0$ gives birth not only to c₀ but to **all three phases at once**. For the same reason, $U_+$ cannot be defined as the global counterpart or outer shell of c⁺.

### Two meanings of the word "Big Bang"

| Meaning | Content |
|---|---|
| **Primordial birth** | The beginning of the universe we observe |
| **Cycle condition** | The birth triggered by the crossing of the threshold at the collapse end of any $U_x$ |

The question whether the primordial birth came from a previous $U_x$ falls within the scope of the **Principle of Silence**.

**Author's ruling — re-triggering.** The re-triggering of the $U_0$ regime does not contradict the ontology of bidirectional arrows. This sentence is not a definition of a cycle mechanism: the mechanism is open and the characterization of $U_0$ remains reserved.

### "Big Rupture" is a homonymous term

In standard cosmology, the *Big Rip* is an event in which dark energy **ends** the universe. In Evertophy, **the Big Rupture is an end and at the same time a new birth** (G-4). A shared word gives no licence to merge the meanings.

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It defines the relation of the Dynamic Universe to the two limiting conditions, and re-formation |
| **Why does it do it?** | It is necessary to settle where the Big Bang and the possible end of the universe stand in the ontology |
| **How does it do it?** | It places the two thresholds (Big Bang, Big Rupture) at the boundaries of the global poles; it separates the result as canon and the mechanism as open; it sets four safeguards |
| **What result is expected?** | That the cycle mechanism can later be established; until it is, that metaphysical speculation stays outside |
| **What result is obtained?** | The five rulings (G-1…G-5) and the Principle of Silence are canon; the mechanism of birth from $U_+$ is open |
## 2.9 What does not hold at the global level

The constraints laid down for the phase level also hold at the global level; a few must be stated explicitly:

- **The metric is not defined at the global level.** $g_{\mu\nu}$ belongs only to Cₘ (Section 9); no metric or tetrad is written for $U_0$, $U_x$, $U_+$.
- **The domain projections of $X$ are not applied to global objects.** $X$ resolves the phase regime; the global poles are not its domain of definition.
- **The language of speed and time is not used.** No ratio, speed or duration is written for global relations.
- **The mathematical type of the global arrows is open.** It is known that they are **not** phase gates; their full types have not been defined.

### Birth ≠ cycle

$$\mathrm{Genesis} = U_0 \rightsquigarrow \{c_0,\, C_m,\, c^+\}_{\text{together, in the same constitution}} \qquad\qquad \mathrm{CycleSeed} = \{\mathcal{L}_{\rm çöküş},\; \mathcal{L}_{\rm seyrelme}\}$$

```latex
\mathrm{Genesis} = U_0 \rightsquigarrow \{c_0,\, C_m,\, c^+\}_{\text{together, in the same constitution}} \qquad\qquad \mathrm{CycleSeed} = \{\mathcal{L}_{\rm çöküş},\; \mathcal{L}_{\rm seyrelme}\}
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Genesis | Birth: the joint formation of the three phases from $U_0$ |
| CycleSeed | Cycle seed: the set of the two limits |
| $\mathcal{L}_{\rm çöküş}$, $\mathcal{L}_{\rm seyrelme}$ | The collapse and dilution limits |
| $\dashrightarrow$ | A link not yet established |

$$\boxed{\;\text{Joint birth} \;\neq\; \text{double-limit cycle mechanism}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{Joint birth} \;\neq\; \text{double-limit cycle mechanism}\;}
```

The full link between the two — $\mathrm{CycleSeed} \dashrightarrow \mathrm{Genesis}$ — **is an open problem**.

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It writes birth and the cycle mechanism as two separate objects |
| **Why does it do it?** | The sentence "if the limit is crossed, a birth occurs" could, before a mechanism is established, be confused with birth itself |
| **How does it do it?** | It defines birth as an operation and the cycle seed as the set of two limits; it leaves the link between them open |
| **What result is expected?** | That the link can later be derived |
| **What result is obtained?** | The distinction is established; the link is an open problem |

## 2.10 The initial mass of the universe and the record of the derivation chain

In the archive, universal-scale estimates are preserved as a chain. This record classifies the numbers not as ontological quantities but as **historical calculations and scale analogies**.

### Links of the chain

1. **CMB scale:** $t_{\rm CMB}\approx3.8\times10^5$ years (recombination) → $D_{\rm çekirdek}\approx3.8\times10^5$ light years. The moment at which we see the CMB is read as the first observational phase transition of Cₘ.
2. **Planck-density projection:** $M\sim\rho_P\cdot V_{\rm çekirdek}\sim10^{161}$ kg in order of magnitude.
3. **Baryon / total correction:** $10^{53}$ kg is only the baryonic mass (~5%); with the Planck 2018 values $\Omega_b\approx5\%$, $\Omega_{\rm DM}\approx27\%$, $\Omega_{\rm DE}\approx68\%$, one obtains $M_{C_m}\sim2\times10^{54}$ kg.
4. **Dirac large numbers:** $D_{\rm evren}/D_{\rm çekirdek}\sim10^{41}$; this ratio relies on a hypothetical model diameter (e.g. $10^{47}$ light years). If the diameter of the observable universe ($\approx9.3\times10^{10}$ light years) is used, the ratio is $\approx2.45\times10^{5}$; the two are not the same input. It is of the same order as the classical Dirac ratios ($F_e/F_g\sim10^{39}$–$10^{40}$, $R_{\rm evren}/r_{\rm proton}\sim10^{40}$).

$$M\sim\rho_P\,V_{\rm çekirdek},\qquad U_0\text{ capacity metaphor}\in\big[\rho_P^{\min}V_{\rm CMB}^{\min},\ \rho_P^{\max}V_{\rm CMB}^{\max}\big]$$

```latex
M\sim\rho_P\,V_{\rm çekirdek},\qquad U_0\text{ capacity metaphor}\in\big[\rho_P^{\min}V_{\rm CMB}^{\min},\ \rho_P^{\max}V_{\rm CMB}^{\max}\big]
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $\rho_P$ | Planck density ($\approx5.16\times10^{96}$ kg/m³) |
| $V_{\rm çekirdek}$, $V_{\rm CMB}$ | The historical core volume derived from the CMB scale |
| $\Omega_b$, $\Omega_{\rm DM}$, $\Omega_{\rm DE}$ | Density fractions of baryons, dark matter and dark energy |
| $D_{\rm evren}$, $D_{\rm çekirdek}$ | Model diameter of the universe and historical core diameter |

### Critical status rulings

$$\boxed{\;10^{161}\ \mathrm{kg}\ \text{is NOT a physical mass; its correct use is as an upper bound / capacity metaphor.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;10^{161}\ \mathrm{kg}\ \text{is NOT a physical mass; its correct use is as an upper bound / capacity metaphor.}\;}
```

$$\boxed{\;\text{A numerical mass reading for } U_0 \text{ is not carried into the current ontology; } U_0 \text{ is neither a phase nor a gate endpoint.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{A numerical mass reading for } U_0 \text{ is not carried into the current ontology; } U_0 \text{ is neither a phase nor a gate endpoint.}\;}
```

$$\boxed{\;\text{The correspondence } 10^{161}\ \leftrightarrow\ 10^{120}\ \text{(cosmological constant) is unsupported and has been withdrawn.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{The correspondence } 10^{161}\ \leftrightarrow\ 10^{120}\ \text{(cosmological constant) is unsupported and has been withdrawn.}\;}
```

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It keeps on record the historical chain of calculations concerning the initial scale of the universe |
| **Why does it do it?** | These numbers occur frequently in the archive; if their status is not stated, they could be mistaken for ontological quantities |
| **How does it do it?** | It writes each link with its source; it states explicitly that the numbers are not physical masses but a capacity metaphor |
| **What result is expected?** | That, once the tree of assumptions is disentangled, it becomes clear which number rests on which input |
| **What result is obtained?** | The convergence of two independent routes to ~$10^{47}$ light years is recorded as a sign of internal consistency; but the number is not taken into the canon. The range-form capacity notation for $U_0$ is not locked until the bands become clear (a safeguard against the risk of "narrative inflation") |




# SECTION 3 — THE THREE-PHASE ONTOLOGICAL ARCHITECTURE

## 3.1 Why three phases?

The three-fold architecture is **motivated** by three distinct boundary tendencies; this does not prove that three-foldness is the only possible ontology. Whether the number of phases is primitive or derived is an open debt (APPENDIX K.1).

- **Downward:** **c₀**, in which differentiation dissolves and matter is integrated into a single structure.
- **Upward:** **c⁺**, in which Cₘ matter support dissolves.
- **In between:** **Cₘ**, in which measurement is possible.

The present framework defines no fourth **independent boundary**. This shows not that a fourth phase is impossible, but that none has been proposed.

**Two extreme tendencies, two global poles.** These two extreme tendencies in the three-phase architecture are related, at the global level, to the **saturation** and **dissolution** poles respectively (Section 2.8). The double-limit structure is not a cosmological assumption added afterwards; it is the global reading of two tendencies already present within the three-phase architecture.

$$\boxed{\;\text{This relation is NOT A ONE-TO-ONE ONTOLOGICAL CORRESPONDENCE of the form } c_0 \leftrightarrow U_0 \text{ or } c^+ \leftrightarrow U_+ \text{.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{This relation is NOT A ONE-TO-ONE ONTOLOGICAL CORRESPONDENCE of the form } c_0 \leftrightarrow U_0 \text{ or } c^+ \leftrightarrow U_+ \text{.}\;}
```

## 3.2 c₀ — the pre-temporal potential / core phase

### Current core definition

**c₀ (Potential / Black-Hole Phase):** The pre-temporal potential and core phase in which measurable matter loses its atomic and particle properties, and all particles merge — to a degree that converges to the metric-gapless state at the Planck scale — into the form of **a single integrated structure**.

| Property | Its state in c₀ |
|---|---|
| Internal physical time | Absent — not yet established ($\bot_0$) |
| Metric $g_{\mu\nu}$ | Not admissible ($\bot_0$) |
| Differentiated matter of Cₘ | Becomes a metric-gapless, contiguous single structure |
| Black hole | Ontologically identical with c₀ (author's ruling) |

### Time in c₀: not stopped, but not yet established

$$\text{There is NO internal physical time in } c_0\text{:}\qquad T(c_0)=\bot_0\neq0$$

```latex
\text{There is NO internal physical time in } c_0\text{:}\qquad T(c_0)=\bot_0\neq0
```

There is a critical distinction here:

| State | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Time **has stopped** | There is a clock, but it does not advance — this is a Cₘ state |
| Time **is absent** | The very concept of a clock cannot be established — this is the state in c₀ |

In c₀, time is **not yet established**; this is why c₀ is called **pre-temporal**. Time does not "flow slowly" there, nor is it "a frozen river". The detailed account is in the description of c₀ in the opening section.

### Matter in c₀

$$\boxed{\;C_m \to c_0: \quad \text{differentiated matter} \;\longrightarrow\; \text{COLLECTIVE SINGLE-STRUCTURE LIMIT}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;C_m \to c_0: \quad \text{differentiated matter} \;\longrightarrow\; \text{COLLECTIVE SINGLE-STRUCTURE LIMIT}\;}
```

The distinction between discrete objects dissolves. The distinction between "this particle" and "that particle" loses its operational content, and the regime acquires a **collective** character.

### The metric in c₀

$g_{\mu\nu}$ is **not an admissible object** in c₀: the metric there is not destroyed, but not yet established.

### Analogies of collective behaviour — the BEC

Bose–Einstein condensation (BEC) and superfluidity are laboratory-accessible examples of **collective and coherent behaviour**, and can be used for the **single-structure intuition** of c₀. By the author's ruling, **the BEC is the environment in the matter phase that is closest to the c₀ environment**; but it is not c₀ itself: time flows inside it and the system remains in Cₘ.

$$\boxed{\;\text{BEC}\neq c_0:\ \text{the environment in the matter phase closest to the } c_0 \text{ environment — an ANALOGY, NOT a literal identity.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{BEC}\neq c_0:\ \text{the environment in the matter phase closest to the } c_0 \text{ environment — an ANALOGY, NOT a literal identity.}\;}
```

### c₀ and black holes

**In the Evertophy ontology, the black hole and the c₀ phase are ontologically identical** (a basic definition directly approved by the author). The quantitative connection of this identity with observables in Cₘ, the mechanism of formation and the relation between the event horizon and the phase boundary must be derived separately; the one-to-one identity "event horizon = phase boundary" has not been established. The ontological identity does not define Cₘ-type time, three-dimensional Euclidean space or a metric inside c₀.

**Author's ruling — a black hole is not an annihilation.** A black hole is neither an annihilation nor an infinite singularity. A particle that touches its surface through the Force of Attraction integrates with the structure, regardless of whether it is entangled; the BEC is here only an analogy of a collective single structure. The particle outside may or may not keep its spin; mass is conserved independently of spin. Spin and entanglement are preserved as long as there is no interaction with the environment (measurement or contact with matter).

**Author's ruling — metric-gapless single structure.** The structure inside the event horizon is **contiguous**, finite and **of a single form**, to a degree that converges to the metric-gapless state at the Planck scale. This does not mean "the black-hole core is Planck-sized"; it says that the mass structure inside the black hole is entirely of a single form, in which almost no metric gap remains between particles. This is the core logic of Evertophy's reading of c₀. The saturation is written through a reading of $X$ ($X$ itself is not a scalar, Section 4):

$$\xi_{\rm sat}=\pi_{\rm sat}(X)\;\longrightarrow\;\xi_{\rm sat}^{\max}\qquad(\text{in the normalized reading }\xi_{\rm sat}\approx1)$$

```latex
\xi_{\rm sat}=\pi_{\rm sat}(X)\;\longrightarrow\;\xi_{\rm sat}^{\max}\qquad(\text{in the normalized reading }\xi_{\rm sat}\approx1)
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $\pi_{\rm sat}$ | The saturation readout projection of $X$ |
| $\xi_{\rm sat}$ | Saturation reading; when normalized, its upper bound is 1 |

**Author's ruling — c₀ capacity.** The internal capacity of c₀ grows in relation to both mass and surface area: capacity scales **linearly with mass**, while the horizon area grows **with the square of the mass** (when the mass doubles, the horizon radius doubles and the horizon area quadruples). Capacity therefore scales with the square root of the horizon area. The ratio between mass and horizon diameter is a known fact. The counting unit of core capacity is the **Planck mass**; the density of the single structure cannot exceed the Planck density. **As a separate author's ruling**, a more massive black hole is not denser; it has a larger volume of the same metric-gapless structure and a wider horizon. This second ruling is not derived from the density upper bound: the upper bound gives only the smallest volume; that density does not increase with mass is a property of the single structure itself (see the conditional scaling below):

$$R_s=\frac{2GM}{c^2},\qquad A=4\pi R_s^2=\frac{16\pi G^2M^2}{c^4}$$

```latex
R_s=\frac{2GM}{c^2},\qquad A=4\pi R_s^2=\frac{16\pi G^2M^2}{c^4}
```

$$N_{c_0}=\frac{M}{m_P},\qquad m_P=\sqrt{\frac{\hbar c}{G}},\qquad \rho_{c_0}\le\rho_P=\frac{c^5}{\hbar G^2},\qquad V_{c_0}\ge\frac{M}{\rho_P}=N_{c_0}\,\ell_P^3$$

```latex
N_{c_0}=\frac{M}{m_P},\qquad m_P=\sqrt{\frac{\hbar c}{G}},\qquad \rho_{c_0}\le\rho_P=\frac{c^5}{\hbar G^2},\qquad V_{c_0}\ge\frac{M}{\rho_P}=N_{c_0}\,\ell_P^3
```

$$R_{c_0}\propto M^{1/3}\ \ (\rho_{c_0}\ \text{constant}),\qquad R_s\propto M,\qquad A\propto M^2,\qquad \frac{R_{c_0}}{R_s}\propto M^{-2/3}$$

```latex
R_{c_0}\propto M^{1/3}\ \ (\rho_{c_0}\ \text{constant}),\qquad R_s\propto M,\qquad A\propto M^2,\qquad \frac{R_{c_0}}{R_s}\propto M^{-2/3}
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $R_s$ | Schwarzschild (horizon) radius |
| $A$ | Horizon surface area |
| $M$ | Mass of the black hole (of the c₀ structure) |
| $G$, $c$, $\hbar$ | Gravitational constant, speed of light, reduced Planck constant |
| $N_{c_0}$ | Core capacity in units of the Planck mass |
| $m_P$ | Planck mass ($\approx2.18\times10^{-8}$ kg) |
| $\rho_P$, $\ell_P$ | Planck density ($\approx5.16\times10^{96}$ kg/m³) and Planck length; $\rho_P\ell_P^3=m_P$ |
| $V_{c_0}$, $R_{c_0}$ | Volume and radius of the single structure |

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It writes the capacity and volume of the single structure inside a black hole, and its scaling with the horizon |
| **Why does it do it?** | A finite and consistent picture must replace the singularity reading of "infinite density, zero volume" |
| **How does it do it?** | It counts capacity in units of the Planck mass; it places the Planck upper bound on density; it scales the volume of the single structure and the horizon with mass |
| **What result is expected?** | A picture in which, as mass increases, it is volume and not density that grows; the establishment of a quantitative form for the relation between horizon area and capacity |
| **What result is obtained?** | The ratios are correct: the horizon radius grows with $M$, the horizon area with $M^2$; the radius of the single structure grows with $M^{1/3}$ **in the constant-density realization**. Taking the density at its Planck value (the smallest radius), for a black hole of one solar mass $R_{c_0}^{\min}\approx4.5\times10^{-23}$ m (much larger than the Planck length, much smaller than the ~3 km horizon); for this reason the horizon and the boundary of the single structure are not regarded as identical. The internal profile and the relation of capacity to the area law are open (APPENDIX K) |

The scaling of capacity with mass, and of horizon area with the square of mass, is consistent with the observationally tested area theorem; the Bekenstein–Hawking area law, which states that entropy is proportional to area, is by contrast a theoretical result not tested by observation (23.3).

### Capacity, density and radius — conditions and type of reading

Of the relations above, one is **derived**, one is a **ruling** and one is a **conditional** result; the three are kept separate:

$$N_{c_0}=\frac{M}{m_P}\propto M\propto\sqrt{A},\qquad \rho_{c_0}\le\rho_P\;\Rightarrow\;V_{c_0}\ge\frac{M}{\rho_P},\qquad R_{c_0}\ge R_{c_0}^{\min}=\left(\frac{3M}{4\pi\rho_P}\right)^{1/3}$$

```latex
N_{c_0}=\frac{M}{m_P}\propto M\propto\sqrt{A},\qquad \rho_{c_0}\le\rho_P\;\Rightarrow\;V_{c_0}\ge\frac{M}{\rho_P},\qquad R_{c_0}\ge R_{c_0}^{\min}=\left(\frac{3M}{4\pi\rho_P}\right)^{1/3}
```

$$\rho_{c_0}(M)=\rho_*\le\rho_P\ \ (\text{constant-density realization})\;\Rightarrow\;R_{c_0}=\left(\frac{3M}{4\pi\rho_*}\right)^{1/3}\propto M^{1/3},\qquad \frac{d\ln R_{c_0}}{d\ln M}=\frac13\left(1-\frac{d\ln\rho_{c_0}}{d\ln M}\right)$$

```latex
\rho_{c_0}(M)=\rho_*\le\rho_P\ \ (\text{constant-density realization})\;\Rightarrow\;R_{c_0}=\left(\frac{3M}{4\pi\rho_*}\right)^{1/3}\propto M^{1/3},\qquad \frac{d\ln R_{c_0}}{d\ln M}=\frac13\left(1-\frac{d\ln\rho_{c_0}}{d\ln M}\right)
```

$$M\in[M^-,M^+],\ \ \rho_{c_0}\in[\rho^-,\rho^+],\ \rho^->0\;\Rightarrow\;R_{c_0}\in\left[\left(\frac{3M^-}{4\pi\rho^+}\right)^{1/3},\ \left(\frac{3M^+}{4\pi\rho^-}\right)^{1/3}\right]$$

```latex
M\in[M^-,M^+],\ \ \rho_{c_0}\in[\rho^-,\rho^+],\ \rho^->0\;\Rightarrow\;R_{c_0}\in\left[\left(\frac{3M^-}{4\pi\rho^+}\right)^{1/3},\ \left(\frac{3M^+}{4\pi\rho^-}\right)^{1/3}\right]
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $N_{c_0}$ | Dimensionless measure of capacity; in general a real number (being an integer would require an additional quantization ruling) |
| $A$ | Horizon area (Schwarzschild family; for a rotating black hole the spin parameter enters separately) |
| $\rho_*$ | Mass-independent density of the single structure (constant-density realization) |
| $R_{c_0}^{\min}$ | The smallest radius, coming from the density upper bound |
| $d\ln R/d\ln M$ | The scaling exponent of radius with mass |
| $M^\pm$, $\rho^\pm$ | The range endpoints of mass and density (independent coverage bands) |

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It separates which of the capacity, density and radius relations is derived, which is a ruling and which is conditional |
| **Why does it do it?** | From the upper bound $\rho\le\rho_P$ alone it does not follow that density is constant or does not increase with mass; this gap in inference was not visible in the earlier account |
| **How does it do it?** | From the upper bound it derives only the smallest volume and radius; it ties $R\propto M^{1/3}$ to the constant-density realization; in the general case it gives the formula tying the scaling exponent to the variation of density with mass (if density does not increase with mass, the exponent is $\ge1/3$) |
| **What result is expected?** | The derivation of the internal profile of the single structure and of $\rho_*$ |
| **What result is obtained?** | $N_{c_0}\propto M\propto\sqrt A$ and $V\ge M/\rho_P$ are **derived**; that density does not increase with mass is an **author's ruling**; $R\propto M^{1/3}$ is a consequence of the **constant-density realization**. The $4.5\times10^{-23}$ m in the solar-mass example is not a measured radius but a lower bound calculated by taking $\rho_*=\rho_P$. If the author's ruling also covers exact proportionality, this means that the mean density is independent of mass ($\rho_{c_0}=\rho_*$) |

**Type of reading (type note).** Since Cₘ-type length and metric are undefined in c₀ ($g_{\mu\nu}|_{c_0}=\bot_0$), $V_{c_0}$, $R_{c_0}$ and $\rho_{c_0}$ are **not the internal geometry** of c₀, but **effective parameters used in the Cₘ physical readout** of the c₀ structure. This is the application to these quantities of the document's distinction between source phase and observation phase (15.1a), and it does not change the identity black hole ≡ c₀:

$$\mathcal O^{C_m}_{c_0}:\ \mathrm{Durum}(c_0)\times\mathrm{Bağlam}(C_m)\longrightarrow\mathcal I(\mathrm{kg})\times\mathcal I(\mathrm{m})\times\mathcal I(\mathrm{m}^3)\times\mathcal I(\mathrm{kg/m^3}),\qquad (M_{c_0},R_{c_0},V_{c_0},\rho_{c_0})=\mathcal O^{C_m}_{c_0}(s,\mathcal C)$$

```latex
\mathcal O^{C_m}_{c_0}:\ \mathrm{Durum}(c_0)\times\mathrm{Bağlam}(C_m)\longrightarrow\mathcal I(\mathrm{kg})\times\mathcal I(\mathrm{m})\times\mathcal I(\mathrm{m}^3)\times\mathcal I(\mathrm{kg/m^3}),\qquad (M_{c_0},R_{c_0},V_{c_0},\rho_{c_0})=\mathcal O^{C_m}_{c_0}(s,\mathcal C)
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $\mathcal O^{C_m}_{c_0}$ | The readout map from the c₀ structure to the effective Cₘ parameters (its form is open) |
| $\mathrm{Durum}(c_0)$, $\mathrm{Bağlam}(C_m)$ | c₀ states and the Cₘ observation context |
| $\mathcal I(D)$ | The range type in unit $D$; the output is the product type of four ranges of different dimensions |
| $s$, $\mathcal C$ | A c₀ state and a Cₘ observation context |
| $M_{c_0}$ | The mass of the c₀ structure as read in Cₘ |

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It states in what sense the volume, radius and density assigned to the metric-free c₀ are defined |
| **Why does it do it?** | For the ruling "length is undefined in c₀" and the statement "the radius of the single structure is so much" to be true together, the type of reading must be explicit |
| **How does it do it?** | It types these quantities not as magnitudes measured inside c₀ but as outputs of a readout map in Cₘ |
| **What result is expected?** | The establishment of the readout map (APPENDIX K.1) |
| **What result is obtained?** | **The type distinction is established**; the map itself is open |

**Author's ruling — departure from c₀.** The speed and/or density of the structure that departs from c₀ determines what will form: matter, light or c⁺. The speed and density here are not internal quantities of c₀ — in c₀ speed, duration and length are undefined —; they are **boundary readings on the Cₘ side** of the transition ($v_\partial$, $\rho_\partial$). The ruling states with which reading the emerging structure appears in Cₘ, and it is read together with the membership band of 3.3:

$$\underbrace{0<v<c}_{C_m\ \text{matter}},\qquad \underbrace{v=c}_{\text{liminal shared boundary (light)}},\qquad c^+:\ \text{dissolution of the band}\ \neq\ v_{c^+}=\frac{dx}{dt}>c$$

```latex
\underbrace{0<v<c}_{C_m\ \text{matter}},\qquad \underbrace{v=c}_{\text{liminal shared boundary (light)}},\qquad c^+:\ \text{dissolution of the band}\ \neq\ v_{c^+}=\frac{dx}{dt}>c
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $v$ | Membership reading; not a laboratory coordinate speed (3.3) |
| $c$ | Speed of light — the consistency constraint of Cₘ |
| $v_{c^+}$ | A Cₘ-type speed for c⁺; not defined (3.4) |

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It defines the criterion that determines to which phase the structure departing from c₀ will go |
| **Why does it do it?** | What the mass and energy coming out of a black hole are read as in Cₘ (matter, light, c⁺) must find a place in the ontology |
| **How does it do it?** | It uses the Cₘ membership band as the criterion: inside the band, matter; on the boundary, light; the regime in which the band dissolves, c⁺ |
| **What result is expected?** | The establishment of a quantitative form of the rule |
| **What result is obtained?** | The ruling is established; that c⁺ is beyond $c$ is not a Cₘ-type superluminal motion (3.4). The quantitative form is open |

**Possibilities left open by the author — capacity in mergers.** When two black holes merge, all possibilities may be open for the core capacity that decreases: (1) a tendency towards c⁺ through gravitational waves, (2) the production of matter, (3) a separate black hole, (4) all of these in part. Which one, or which combination, is realized is open.

### Historical over-concrete definitions

The following are at the **historical or model** level and are not the current core: the stasis lock · the zero-differential engine. The old readings "full Planck saturation state" and "a definite Planck density", in turn, have been rewritten in current form by the author's ruling of a metric-gapless single structure: there is no claim that the core is locked to a definite numerical density; there is only the upper bound $\rho_{c_0}\le\rho_P$.

The notations "not infinite density, but zero differential" and `dt=0, dx=0` are not current ontological definitions. But neither are they regarded as entirely deleted: they may be mentioned as **historical-pedagogical shorthands** expressing that ordinary Cₘ freedoms of time and space are not admissible in c₀.

$$\boxed{\;\text{In } c_0\text{, Cₘ-type }dt\text{ and }dx\text{ are not ontological primitives; "}dt=0,\ dx=0\text{" is only a pedagogical boundary language.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{In } c_0\text{, Cₘ-type }dt\text{ and }dx\text{ are not ontological primitives; "}dt=0,\ dx=0\text{" is only a pedagogical boundary language.}\;}
```

This shorthand does not mean that a previously established clock inside c₀ has stopped, or that the Cₘ metric has been set to zero.

### Temporal position in c₀

c₀ is **not a temporal priority but a position of regime**. Within the current $U_x$, too, c₀ membership may be possible in high-density regimes; which physical system carries c₀ membership depends on the quantitative bridge and is an open debt.

**Author's ruling — local re-formation.** The phases are formed together at the Big Bang; in addition, matter that succumbs to the Force of Attraction at a critical mass can locally form c₀ again, and extreme dissolution (entropy / disorder) can form c⁺. These are the Cₘ → c₀ and Cₘ → c⁺ edges of the phase triangle; the quantitative form of the thresholds is open.

### Temperature in c₀ and absolute zero

**Author's ruling:** $c_0\neq$ absolute zero. Absolute zero (0 K) is the thermodynamic limit state within the Cₘ phase itself; in c₀, by contrast, the very concepts of temperature and heat are undefined.

$$T(c_0)=\bot_0\neq0,\qquad T\to0\ \mathrm K\ \Rightarrow\ C_m\ \text{ground state}$$

```latex
T(c_0)=\bot_0\neq0,\qquad T\to0\ \mathrm K\ \Rightarrow\ C_m\ \text{ground state}
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $T$ | Temperature — a quantity defined only in Cₘ |
| $\bot_0$ | The "not yet established" absence in c₀ (5.3); not the numerical zero |
| $0\ \mathrm K$ | Absolute zero: the thermodynamic limit of Cₘ |

A system approaching absolute zero remains in Cₘ: quantum zero-point motion continues and clocks keep running. Cooling alone does not lead to c₀; what is approached in ultra-cold experiments (the BEC) is not c₀ but the ground state of Cₘ.

Read together with the ruling black hole ≡ c₀, the Hawking temperature attributed to a black hole is interpreted not as the internal temperature of c₀, but as the thermal reading that the horizon gives to a distant Cₘ observer. This is a **candidate reading**; taking it into the canon is the author's decision, and the quantitative bridge is open (APPENDIX K.2).

$$T_H=\frac{\hbar c^3}{8\pi G k_B M}$$

```latex
T_H=\frac{\hbar c^3}{8\pi G k_B M}
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $T_H$ | Hawking temperature — the Cₘ readout temperature of the horizon (candidate reading) |
| $k_B$ | Boltzmann constant |
| $M$ | Black-hole mass |

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It establishes the relation of c₀ to temperature: it is not absolute zero; temperature is undefined there |
| **Why does it do it?** | The misconception "c₀ = the coldest state" confused a limiting state of Cₘ with c₀ |
| **How does it do it?** | It separates absolute zero as the ground state of Cₘ, and c₀ as the regime in which temperature is not established; it interprets the Hawking temperature as the reading of the horizon in Cₘ |
| **What result is expected?** | The establishment of a quantitative bridge between the Hawking temperature and the internal structure of c₀ |
| **What result is obtained?** | "c₀ ≠ absolute zero" is an author's ruling; the interpretation of the Hawking temperature as a horizon reading is a candidate reading |
## 3.3 Cₘ — differentiated matter / measurement / emergent time

### Current core definition

**Cₘ (Matter / Atom / Particle Phase):** The phase in which the physical reality we directly interact with exists: differentiated massive matter, atoms and subatomic particles, measurement processes, and the time we experience as a unidirectional flow.

| Property | Its state in Cₘ |
|---|---|
| Matter | Differentiated matter |
| Measurement | Direct physical measurement only here |
| Time | The **only home** of emergent physical time |
| Metric | The **only domain of definition** of $g_{\mu\nu}$ |

Standard physical speeds, ratios and metric readings are meaningful **only here**.

### Cₘ and the light barrier

$$\boxed{\;\text{Before it is a SPEED LIMIT, } c \text{ is the CONSISTENCY CONSTRAINT of Cₘ.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{Before it is a SPEED LIMIT, } c \text{ is the CONSISTENCY CONSTRAINT of Cₘ.}\;}
```

$c$ sets the limit of information processing, record and resolution of the Cₘ phase. It is read not as the speed limit of an object moving in space, but as the limit of the phase's own internal consistency.

### The kinematic membership band

$$\boxed{\;\text{The matter-membership condition of Cₘ is the sustainable speed band } 0<v<c\text{; } v=c \text{ is the liminal phase boundary.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{The matter-membership condition of Cₘ is the sustainable speed band } 0<v<c\text{; } v=c \text{ is the liminal phase boundary.}\;}
```

This is the author's core ruling. The $v$ here is not the local laboratory coordinate speed: the ontological status of the band is definite; the precise mathematical reading of $v$ and its connection with measurement are open.

$$\underbrace{0<v<c}_{\text{core membership ruling}} \;+\; \underbrace{\mathcal R_v:\ \text{state} \to v_{\rm üyelik}}_{\text{open reading}}, \qquad v_{\rm lab}=0 \;\not\Rightarrow\; c_0$$

```latex
\underbrace{0<v<c}_{\text{core membership ruling}} \;+\; \underbrace{\mathcal R_v:\ \text{state} \to v_{\rm üyelik}}_{\text{open reading}}, \qquad v_{\rm lab}=0 \;\not\Rightarrow\; c_0
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $v$ | Membership reading: the joint reading of an object's spatial position, overall motion and internal dynamics |
| $\mathcal R_v$ | The readout map from a state to the membership speed; open |
| $v_{\rm lab}$ | Local laboratory speed |

The "0" at the lower end of the band is a **relative-speed floor** defined with respect to any cosmological or inertial reference frame. Even a particle at rest in the laboratory is a member of Cₘ because of its internal dynamics, spin structure and cosmological drift. The expression "universal reference" is not a classical privileged-frame coordinate speed; the membership reading is at the level of the $X$-resolution (Section 4). The mathematical reference object of the band is deliberately left open until the readout map (15.2) and the realization of $X$ (24.3) are closed. The rule of Section 3.7 ("map position ≠ phase membership") also holds for kinematic membership: the measured local speed does not by itself determine phase membership. Structures inside Cₘ are classified not only by the kinematic band but by the multi-constraint resolution in the **interior region** of the phase-parameter landscape (Section 12.3).

**Crossing the band is not an acceleration.** Evertophy accepts the experimentally verified relativistic results in Cₘ as physical constraints that must be explained; their derivation from the emergent structure must be established separately. A massive body inside Cₘ cannot reach $c$ by accelerating. The transition to c⁺ is a **phase transition** that takes place at the threshold $v=c$: what is crossed is not a speed value but the membership band of Cₘ; after the transition, the speed language of Cₘ is undefined.

**Light is liminal — a shared boundary.** The photon lies on the $v=c$ boundary: it is not a member of the band but the liminal state of the boundary. This boundary is the **shared boundary** of Cₘ and c⁺: a structure on the boundary of the Cₘ band is at the same time on the boundary of the c⁺ regime. Lying on the boundary is not the same as being a member of either phase; the photon is a member neither of the massive matter of Cₘ nor of c⁺ (7.6).

$$\text{boundary}(C_m)\big|_{v=c}\;=\;\text{boundary}(c^+)\big|_{C_m\text{ side}},\qquad \text{liminal}\neq C_m\ \text{membership},\qquad \text{liminal}\neq c^+\ \text{membership}$$

```latex
\text{boundary}(C_m)\big|_{v=c}\;=\;\text{boundary}(c^+)\big|_{C_m\text{ side}},\qquad \text{liminal}\neq C_m\ \text{membership},\qquad \text{liminal}\neq c^+\ \text{membership}
```

**Author's ruling — gravitational waves.** Gravitational waves, like light, propagate on the phase regime–threshold boundary, that is, on the shared boundary $v=c$; this does not mean c⁺ membership (7.6). It is known from measurement that gravitational waves propagate at the same speed as light (GW170817); the ruling is consistent with this measurement, but not derived from it.

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It determines when a structure is a member of Cₘ as matter, and where light stands |
| **Why does it do it?** | Confusing phase membership with local speed (e.g. "$v_{\rm lab}=0$ implies c₀") and readings of superluminal matter would break the ontology |
| **How does it do it?** | It fixes the membership band as $0<v<c$; it defines $v=c$ as the shared liminal boundary of Cₘ and c⁺; it separates $v$ as a membership reading |
| **What result is expected?** | The establishment of the readout map $\mathcal R_v$ and the recovery of relativistic results from the emergent structure |
| **What result is obtained?** | The band and the shared-boundary reading are canon; $\mathcal R_v$ and the mathematical reference object of the band are open |

### Cₘ and measurement

$$\boxed{\;\text{Everything said about } c_0 \text{ and } c^+ \text{ is a READING made from Cₘ.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{Everything said about } c_0 \text{ and } c^+ \text{ is a READING made from Cₘ.}\;}
```

### Cₘ is the epistemic centre — not a topologically mandatory intermediary

This distinction is critical. Cₘ is central from the point of view of **measurement and record**. In the phase topology, passing through Cₘ is not mandatory (Section 3.5).

### Note on the model layer

The following records are **models and candidate bridges** inherited from the historical development layer. They aim at computability and testability; none of them redefines Postulate 0, the three-phase ontology, the core definition of $X$ or the stamp algebra. The distinction between model and ontology is preserved exactly as in the epistemic order of Section 1.5 and APPENDIX E.

### Scale-regime working classes — candidate model

$$\boxed{\;\text{The quantum, thermal, astrophysical and cosmological classes are SCALE-REGIME classes within Cₘ; they are not ontological phases.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{The quantum, thermal, astrophysical and cosmological classes are SCALE-REGIME classes within Cₘ; they are not ontological phases.}\;}
```

| Scale-regime class | Typical working domain |
|---|---|
| **Quantum** | Subatomic systems |
| **Thermal** | Laboratory and many-body thermal systems |
| **Astrophysical** | Stellar, compact-object and galactic scales |
| **Cosmological** | The scale of the universe |

These classes are **not** the ontological phases c₀, Cₘ and c⁺. They may be used to group the rows of the phase-parameter matrix, or families of observables, by scale; they do not form a separate ontological layer.

## 3.4 c⁺ — the atemporal upper / archive / coordination phase

### Current core definition

**c⁺ (Dark Matter / Dark Energy / Vacuum Phase — Dissolved Phase):** All of the structure of the universe that is not in the form of matter (the entire particle structure) or of black holes: cosmic voids, the vacuum medium, dark matter and dark energy — and **even the space between the particles that make up the atom** (author's ruling).

| Property | Its state in c⁺ |
|---|---|
| Internal Cₘ physical time | Absent — dissolved ($\bot_+$) |
| Internal Cₘ speed | Undefined |
| Metric $g_{\mu\nu}$ | Absent ($\bot_+$) |
| Cₘ matter support | Dissolves |
| Ontological identity | Dark matter ≡ dark energy ≡ vacuum |
| The future | c⁺ is not an oracle of the future |

### The distinctive property of c⁺

$$\boxed{\;\mathrm{supp}_{C_m}(\text{matter}) \to \varnothing,\qquad g_{\mu\nu}^{(C_m)}\to\bot_+\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\mathrm{supp}_{C_m}(\text{matter}) \to \varnothing,\qquad g_{\mu\nu}^{(C_m)}\to\bot_+\;}
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $\mathrm{supp}_{C_m}(\text{matter})$ | Cₘ matter support: the basis on which Cₘ-type matter can exist |
| $\varnothing$ | The empty set |
| $g_{\mu\nu}^{(C_m)}$ | The Cₘ metric |
| $\bot_+$ | The "dissolved" absence in c⁺ |

**The dissolution of Cₘ matter support** is the main ontological distinction of c⁺.

### Cₘ concepts are not carried into c⁺ unchanged

| Not used | Current safe statement |
|---|---|
| ~~matter carrying Cₘ-type rest mass remains unchanged in c⁺~~ | Cₘ matter support dissolves |
| ~~diluted length~~ | Cₘ metric length is not admissible in c⁺ |
| ~~diluted duration~~ | Cₘ physical duration is not admissible in c⁺ |
| ~~$v>c$, superluminal matter~~ | The internal Cₘ speed language is undefined |
| ~~the outer shell moves faster than light~~ | The concept of motion belongs to the Cₘ reading |

This safeguard prevents **the direct carrying of the Cₘ matter ontology into c⁺**.

#### The historical K2 subtlety

In one version of the old formal core, an abstract "mass dimension" carrier was encoded as permitted for c⁺ (`actM cp = true`). This record is preserved as formal provenance information; but since the same old specification also carries other assumptions that are invalid today, this line on its own does not count as current physical canon.

$$\boxed{\;\text{Cₘ material / rest-mass support is not preserved in c⁺}\qquad \text{the abstract mass-dimension record of historical K2}\;\neq\;\text{a current c⁺-specific mass observable}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{Cₘ material / rest-mass support is not preserved in c⁺}\qquad \text{the abstract mass-dimension record of historical K2}\;\neq\;\text{a current c⁺-specific mass observable}\;}
```

That the dark sector produces a gravitational reading in Cₘ does not prove that there is a Cₘ-type mass carrier inside c⁺. According to Postulate 0, what stands in a more fundamental position than matter is the Force of Attraction; gravitation is one of its metric projections in Cₘ.

### The matter ontologies of c₀ and c⁺ are not the same

$$\boxed{\;C_m\to c_0:\text{ metric-gapless single structure}\qquad C_m\to c^+:\text{ dissolution of Cₘ matter support}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;C_m\to c_0:\text{ metric-gapless single structure}\qquad C_m\to c^+:\text{ dissolution of Cₘ matter support}\;}
```

In the first, differentiated matter goes to **a single collective structure**; in the second, Cₘ matter support **dissolves**.

### c⁺ and the archive

The characteristic property of c⁺ is its character as **archive and coordination**. The records produced in Cₘ are sealed there and **are present together without requiring temporal ordering**. The expression "the whole past is present at the same time" is not used, because "at the same time" is a concept of physical time.

$$\boxed{\;\text{Sealed records of Cₘ origin are present together without requiring temporal ordering.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{Sealed records of Cₘ origin are present together without requiring temporal ordering.}\;}
```

Being present together does not mean being accessible from Cₘ; access from Cₘ depends on an explicit readout law (15.2).

### Time in c⁺

In c⁺ there is no time, and no constraint that depends on time. This is a different absence from the "not yet established" absence in c₀: in c⁺, the time that had been established in Cₘ is **dissolved**. Writing "$dt_{c^+}=0$" is, as in c₀, only a pedagogical shorthand; the ontological statement is $\bot_+$, and $\bot_+$ is not the numerical zero.

$$T(c^+)=\bot_+,\qquad \mathcal G_{C_m}\big|_{c^+}=\bot_+\neq0,\qquad dt_{c^+}=0\ \ (\text{pedagogical shorthand only}),\qquad \bot_0\neq\bot_+\neq0$$

```latex
T(c^+)=\bot_+,\qquad \mathcal G_{C_m}\big|_{c^+}=\bot_+\neq0,\qquad dt_{c^+}=0\ \ (\text{pedagogical shorthand only}),\qquad \bot_0\neq\bot_+\neq0
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $T(c^+)$ | Physical time in c⁺ |
| $\mathcal G_{C_m}$ | The structure of distinguishable change in Cₘ (7.2) |
| $\bot_0$, $\bot_+$ | The "not yet established" absence in c₀ and the "dissolved" absence in c⁺ |

### c⁺ and the speed of light

The relation of c⁺ to the speed of light is locked by three sentences:

$$\boxed{\;c^+\text{ is the beyond-}c\text{ ontological phase that lies beyond the } c\text{-bounded space-time regime of Cₘ.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;c^+\text{ is the beyond-}c\text{ ontological phase that lies beyond the } c\text{-bounded space-time regime of Cₘ.}\;}
```

$$\boxed{\;c^+\text{ being beyond } c \;\not\equiv\; \text{a Cₘ-type } v_{c^+}=\frac{dx}{dt}>c\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;c^+\text{ being beyond } c \;\not\equiv\; \text{a Cₘ-type } v_{c^+}=\frac{dx}{dt}>c\;}
```

$$\boxed{\;\text{beyond-}c\text{ ontological regime} \;\neq\; \text{ordinary superluminal matter motion measured inside Cₘ}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{beyond-}c\text{ ontological regime} \;\neq\; \text{ordinary superluminal matter motion measured inside Cₘ}\;}
```

The reason for the second and third sentences is this: in c⁺, the physical time, three-dimensional Euclidean space, metric and differentiated matter support specific to Cₘ are dissolved; consequently, a Cₘ speed of the form $dx/dt$ is not defined there. The author's ruling is this: **c⁺ is the upper phase, outside the band $0<v<c$, in which the structure of matter cannot be preserved once the light-speed threshold is crossed; it is at the same time the timeless phase in which the temporal record and order information formed in Cₘ are sealed; physical time itself does not exist in c⁺.** c⁺ is formed at the $U_0$ birth together with c₀ and Cₘ, in the same constitution (2.8). What remains open is only the final mathematical representation of being beyond $c$. Statements about c⁺ and speed in the other sections of the document are to be read according to this ruling.

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It makes precise what it means for c⁺ to be "beyond the speed of light" |
| **Why does it do it?** | A reading of "faster-than-light matter" would contradict both the measurements and the absence of time and space in c⁺ |
| **How does it do it?** | It defines being beyond $c$ as a difference of regime; it states that the Cₘ-type speed expression cannot be applied to c⁺ |
| **What result is expected?** | The establishment of a final mathematical representation of being beyond $c$ |
| **What result is obtained?** | The three locking sentences are canon; the mathematical representation is open |

### c⁺ ≡ vacuum, c⁺ ≠ the future

$$\boxed{\;c^+\equiv\text{Dark Matter}\equiv\text{Dark Energy}\equiv\text{Vacuum}\qquad c^+\neq\text{the future}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;c^+\equiv\text{Dark Matter}\equiv\text{Dark Energy}\equiv\text{Vacuum}\qquad c^+\neq\text{the future}\;}
```

**Author's ruling — c⁺ is equivalent to the vacuum.** Ontologically, c⁺ is the vacuum itself. The "vacuum state" of quantum field theory, and the calculations made with it, are **the computational language within Cₘ** of this identity; every effect measured in the vacuum (for example, the Casimir force) is accepted unconditionally and is interpreted in Evertophy as the reading of c⁺ in Cₘ. **The representation and what it represents are separate layers:** the vacuum state $|0\rangle$ of quantum field theory and the calculations of vacuum energy are a Cₘ computational representation; the ontological object they represent is c⁺. This distinction does not weaken the identity (third principle): c⁺ ≡ vacuum is an ontological identity, while $|0\rangle$ is its mathematical representation in Cₘ.

**Author's ruling — space inside the atom.** Even the space between the particles that make up the atom is c⁺. Since three-dimensional space is dissolved in c⁺, it is not correct to say "c⁺ is located somewhere"; the correct statement is that every region Cₘ reads as "empty space" is the reading of c⁺ in Cₘ. This ruling does not change the measured results of atomic physics; the predictions of quantum mechanics inside the atom remain fully valid as the computational language of Cₘ.

$$\boxed{\;\text{Every region read as "empty space" on the }C_m\text{ screen — from intergalactic voids to the space between particles inside the atom — is the }C_m\text{ reading of }c^+\text{.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{Every region read as "empty space" on the }C_m\text{ screen — from intergalactic voids to the space between particles inside the atom — is the }C_m\text{ reading of }c^+\text{.}\;}
```

Nor is c⁺ a source of prophecy or a controllable superluminal communication channel.

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It defines the relation of c⁺ to the vacuum and to the empty space within matter |
| **Why does it do it?** | That the dark sector, the vacuum and the empty space inside matter appeared to be things requiring separate explanations concealed different readings of the same phase |
| **How does it do it?** | It holds c⁺ to be ontologically equivalent to the vacuum; it positions QFT vacuum calculations as the Cₘ computational language of this identity; it counts every place that Cₘ reads as "empty space" as a reading of c⁺ |
| **What result is expected?** | That vacuum effects and the dark-matter and dark-energy readings are connected quantitatively under a single phase identity |
| **What result is obtained?** | The identity and the ruling on space inside the atom are author's rulings; the quantitative mechanism for each observational phenomenon is open (11.2) |

### Historical language

The following are **historical or analogical**, not the current core: "the cocoon of the universe" · "graph state" · "outer shell" · "texture seeping into inner space".

## 3.5 Phase topology — the complete bidirectional triangle
