# SECTION 27 — FUNDAMENTAL EQUATIONS AND THE BRIDGE FORMALISM (CANDIDATE INVENTORY)

This section takes an inventory, at the level of names, of the family of bridge equations in the archive. It is an extension of the core protective records of APPENDIX C; it contains no new numerical result. The detailed explanation of symbols and the question–answer table for each equation are in the section shown in parentheses; here the equations are brought together collectively, in two layers according to their levels of confidence.

That an equation appears in this section does not mean that it is stated with the same confidence. The section is therefore divided into two layers: verified structural equations (27.A), and model and bridge equations whose scope and assumptions are written explicitly (27.B). The historical and candidate inventory coming from the archive, and the families of models, are kept in the V24 rolling master record.

## 27.A Verified structural equations

These equations establish **the structural consistency** of the framework; they are not physical predictions.

| Equation | What it says | Kind of proof |
|---|---|---|
| $g = E^{\mathsf T}\eta E,\quad \det g = -(\det E)^2$ | The algebraic condition of non-degeneracy; that the metric is permitted only in Cₘ **is a separate phase postulate** (Section 9) | Symbolic — two independent computation engines |
| $w\cdot w = w \iff w \in \{e,\ \kappa_{0m},\ \kappa_{+m}\}$ | The idempotents of the stamp algebra; associativity, termination, unique normal form and confluence are also proven (Section 14) | Formal (Lean) |
| $x\in[a,b],\ y\in[c,d] \Rightarrow xy \in [\min(ac,ad,bc,bd),\ \max(ac,ad,bc,bd)]$ | The reliability of range arithmetic; addition, negation, intersection and exclusion of zero are also proven (6.4a) | Formal (Lean) |
| $q \text{ gate-locked} \iff q \text{ natively defined in at most one phase}$ | That Cₘ-specific quantities cannot be transported through any gate (13.1a) | Formal (Lean) |
| $\text{Type}+\text{range}+\text{well-definedness} \nRightarrow \text{a unique bridge}$ | $B_1(x)=x$ and $B_2(x)=x+\tfrac{x(1-x)}{2}$ satisfy the same conditions (24.1) | Formal (finite) + analytic (continuous) |
| $N_{\rm arşiv}(n+1) = N_{\rm arşiv}(n) + 1$ | The discrete monotonicity of the archive count; not a derivative with respect to physical time (Section 14) | Formal (Lean) |
| $\text{Hard rejection} \iff B_{\rm pred}\cap B_{\rm obs}=\varnothing$ | The falsification criterion; a measure of agreement alone is not a reason for rejection (21.6) | Formal (Lean) |

LaTeX record of the equations:

```latex
g = E^{\mathsf T}\eta E,\quad \det g = -(\det E)^2
w\cdot w = w \iff w \in \{e,\ \kappa_{0m},\ \kappa_{+m}\}
x\in[a,b],\ y\in[c,d] \Rightarrow xy \in [\min(ac,ad,bc,bd),\ \max(ac,ad,bc,bd)]
q \text{ gate-locked} \iff q \text{ natively defined in at most one phase}
\text{Type}+\text{range}+\text{well-definedness} \nRightarrow \text{a unique bridge}
N_{\rm arşiv}(n+1) = N_{\rm arşiv}(n) + 1
\text{Hard rejection} \iff B_{\rm pred}\cap B_{\rm obs}=\varnothing
```

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| $g$, $E$, $\eta$ | Metric, tetrad, Minkowski metric (9.2) |
| $w$, $e$, $\kappa_{0m}$, $\kappa_{+m}$ | Stamp word, unit, the two lock stamps (14.2) |
| $[a,b]$, $[c,d]$ | Closed ranges |
| $q$ | A kind of quantity |
| $B_1$, $B_2$ | Two candidate bridges satisfying the same formal conditions |
| $N_{\rm arşiv}(n)$ | The number of records after the $n$-th append |
| $B_{\rm pred}$, $B_{\rm obs}$ | Prediction and observation bands |

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It gathers into a single table the formally proven structural results of the framework |
| **Why does it do it?** | So that the reader can tell at a glance which results have really been proven and which are models |
| **How does it do it?** | It lists each equation with what it says and with its kind of proof (symbolic, Lean, analytic) |
| **What result is expected?** | That the physical bridges to be built on this structural core rest on a consistent foundation |
| **What result is obtained?** | All are **proven structural results**; none is a physical prediction, and none contributes to the counter |

## 27.B Model and bridge equations with written scope

These equations are correct within their own assumptions; they are not promoted to the core.

| Equation | Scope and assumption | Status |
|---|---|---|
| $\int \mathcal G_{\rm FS}\,d\lambda \ge \theta_{\min},\quad \Delta t\ge\hbar\theta_{\min}/\overline{\Delta E}$ | Quantum-Cₘ only; pure state, unitary evolution, $\overline{\Delta E}>0$; it is a lower bound (7.4) | Bridge |
| $\tau_0\tilde\Gamma\,\mathcal G_{C_m}=\eta_{\rm clock}\,d\tau_{\rm GR}/d\lambda$ | Candidate clock mapping; for a static observer $d\tau_{\rm GR}=\sqrt{-g_{00}}\,dt_{\rm koor}$ (7.4) | Bridge |
| $G_{\mu\nu}+\Lambda g_{\mu\nu} = \frac{8\pi G}{c^4}\,T_{\mu\nu}[J_X]$ | The standard Cₘ comparison base; $J_X$ is an open debt (24.1) | Bridge |
| $-\nabla\cdot(Z\nabla\phi)+\mu^2\phi+4\pi G\rho=0,\quad m=\mu/\sqrt{Z}$ | Constant $Z>0$ and suitable boundary / coercivity conditions; in the pure Neumann case $\mu=0$ an additional mean condition is required | Model — weak-field test bed |
| $Q(m) = e^{-m(r_2-r_1)}\,\frac{1+mr_2}{1+mr_1}$ | $0<r_1<r_2$, $m>0$; strictly decreasing; the inverse problem gives $m$ ($\mu=m\sqrt Z$ only if $Z$ is known) | Model |
| $\sigma^* = 1/\sqrt2,\quad \sigma_{\rm fold}\approx 0.7391346294$ | The regime thresholds of the ideal equilateral Gaussian landscape (19.1a) | Model |
| At the mathematical ends $z\to\pm\infty$, $w\to-1 \iff (X_{\min}>0 \lor p=0)\wedge(X_{\max}<1 \lor q=0)$ | Logistic readout model; $s>0$, $p,q$ finite; the source derivation is open. Physical domain $z>-1$, physical future $z\to-1^+$ (11.9) | Model |
| $\chi_S \equiv 1,\quad \Delta\varphi \equiv 0$ (reciprocal system) | Same port normalization, $S_{21}=S_{12}\neq0$ (17.1) | Limiting result |
| $\vert A_{\rm loop}\vert = \dfrac{\pi\vert k\vert\delta^2\omega}{\lambda^2+\omega^2}$ | Relaxational linear-response model, steady periodic regime; the $1/\omega$ form only for $\omega\gg\lambda$ | Model |
| $\Delta_{0m}\,\Delta_{+m} = \dfrac{d\ln F}{d\tau}$ | An identity following from the chain rule; the gain relation $\Delta_{0+} = G\,\Delta_{+m}$, which is the physical claim, is separate | Identity correct / gain a model |
| $\ln\dfrac{Z(\tau)}{Z(\tau_0)} = -\displaystyle\int_{\tau_0}^{\tau}\eta(u)\,[F(u)-F^*(u)]\,du$ | Drift-impedance model; $\alpha^{-1}\propto Z_{\rm drift}$ ($\xi=1$) is definitional | Model |
| $N_{c_0}=M/m_P,\ \ \rho_{c_0}\le\rho_P,\ \ R_{c_0}\propto M^{1/3}$ | The capacity relations of the c₀ single structure; the radius scaling only in the constant-density ($\rho_*\le\rho_P$) realization; $R,V,\rho$ are effective Cₘ readings (Section 3) | Author's ruling (relations) / internal profile open |
| $\Psi_{0+}=\ln\dfrac{\Lambda c^4\ell^4}{6G^2M^2},\ \ \mathcal A=\dfrac1w\ln\!\Big(1+\dfrac{m_P^2}{2M^2}\Big)$ | The thought-experiment rate model of the direct c₀ ⇄ c⁺ edge lock (13.6) | Model |

# APPENDIX A — GLOSSARY OF CONCEPTS

| Concept | Definition |
|---|---|
| **Force of Attraction (Turkish: Çekim Kuvveti)** | The fundamental ontological postulate; phase-independent constraint coherence. The four known forces are its projections according to scale, amount of mass and phase. Not to be confused with "gravitation" |
| **Gravitation (Turkish: Kütleçekim)** | The metric projection of the Force of Attraction in Cₘ; the subject of general relativity |
| **Constraint coherence** | The necessity that the permitted configurations of the three phases be consistent with one another; it does not require a metric |
| **Clustered / expansion-sustaining c⁺** | The appearances of c⁺ clustered with matter (dark matter) and, far from the pressure of matter, sustaining expansion (dark energy); the archive names "bound / free" correspond to these |
| **Vacuum** | The Cₘ reading of c⁺ at laboratory and intra-atomic scale; **c⁺ ≡ vacuum** (author's ruling). The vacuum state of quantum field theory is the Cₘ computational language of this identity |
| **Intra-atomic space** | The space between the particles that make up the atom; it is the Cₘ reading of c⁺ (author's ruling) |
| **Liminal** | Lying on the boundary $v=c$; the state of light. It is the shared boundary of Cₘ and c⁺; it is membership neither in the band $0<v<c$ nor in c⁺ (boundary ≠ membership) |
| **Kinematic membership band** | The matter-membership band $0<v<c$ of Cₘ; $v=c$ is the liminal shared boundary |
| **$U_0$ (Absolute Core)** | The pre-layer / absolute generative source that forms the three phases together, in the same constitution; the layer in which all the structures of the Dynamic Universe exist in a single form, contiguous to a degree that converges to the metric-gapless state; not a phase |
| **$U_x$ (Dynamic Universe)** | The global structure in which the three phases are realized together; neither a fourth phase nor $X$ |
| **$U_+$ (Absolute Dissolution ≡ Absolute Rupture)** | The global dissolution pole; when the whole $U_x$ structure reaches this form, Absolute Rupture and Absolute Dissolution express the same thing. Not a phase, not $c^+$ |
| **Global universe level** | The layer $U_0$, $U_x$, $U_+$; separate from the phase level |
| **Principle of Silence** | Where there is no representation, Evertophy passes no judgement |
| **Phase regime–threshold boundary** | The transition band on the edges; not a new phase, gate or node |
| **SYNTAX** | Candidate system of rules determining valid configurations; its relation with $U_x$ / $X$ is open |
| **Reserved ontological heading** | A structure whose addition has been decided but whose meaning has not yet been written |
| **Big Rupture** | The event, both an end and a new birth, that takes place when the global $U_x$ crosses the critical threshold at the boundary of $U_+$; not the terminal Big Rip scenario of standard cosmology |
| **c₀ (Potential / Black-Hole Phase)** | The pre-temporal potential and core phase; black hole ≡ c₀ (author's ruling). Matter is in the form of a contiguous, finite single structure, to a degree that converges to the metric-gapless state at the Planck scale. Time has not stopped; it **has not yet been established** |
| **Metric-gapless single structure** | The integrated single form of matter in c₀, in which almost no metric gap remains between particles; not a Planck-sized point; it grows with mass ($\rho\le\rho_P$) |
| **Cₘ (Matter Phase)** | The phase of differentiated matter; the only home of measurement and physical time; its base space is flat, three-dimensional Euclidean space |
| **c⁺ (Dark Matter / Dark Energy / Vacuum Phase)** | The atemporal upper / archive / coordination phase; Cₘ matter support dissolves; c⁺ ≡ DM ≡ DE ≡ Vacuum |
| **BEC** | Bose–Einstein condensation; not c₀; **the environment in the matter phase closest to the c₀ environment** |
| **$X(x)$** | Dynamic meta-resolver; resolves the phase regime and the permitted values; not a scalar; its readings are $\xi_k=\pi_k(X)$ |
| **Postulate 0** | That the Force of Attraction is the fundamental ontological postulate |
| **Range Formalism** | Writing physical quantities as interval-valued; $[P,P]$ is valid |
| **Gate** | The possibility of phase transition; bidirectional |
| **Transport permission** | That a quantity can pass through a gate; it requires the quantity to be natively defined at both ends |
| **Readout** | The observation in Cₘ, in Cₘ's own language, of an effect sourced in c⁺ or c₀; not transport |
| **Stamp** | The record imprinted in a transition; unidirectional |
| **Sealing** | The irreversible writing of a record |
| **Record channel** | The unidirectional Cₘ → c⁺ channel; once sealed, the record is fixed (author's ruling) |
| **Phase memory** | The monotone accumulation of threshold statistics in provenance order |
| **Phase-parameter matrix** | PropertyFamily × Phase → Range or ⊥ |
| **⊥₀ / ⊥₊** | The "not yet established" undefinedness in c₀ and the "dissolved" undefinedness in c⁺; the two are different and neither is the numerical zero |
| **Threshold (liminal) regime** | An intermediate state not fully projected onto Cₘ |
| **Parameter budget** | The rule free inputs < predicted outputs |
| **Intersection measure** | The overlap ratio of prediction and observation ranges |
| **Compatible reading** | An Evertophy interpretation that does not contradict the measured results of an experiment; not a claim of certification or of being the only explanation |
| **Counter** | The canonical numerical-prediction counter; current value 0/10 |
| **Consensus ⇏ canon** | The consensus of multiple systems does not determine the canon; only the author determines the canon |

# APPENDIX C — FUNDAMENTAL EQUATIONS AND PROTECTIVE RECORDS

This appendix has been prepared to prevent the same equation symbol from drifting into different meanings in different layers. The equations are divided into thematic groups; each group is followed by a short symbol table and a question–answer table. Detailed explanations are in the sections shown in parentheses; all symbols are also collected in APPENDIX B. The distinction between **core**, **formal candidate** and **model** is stated explicitly in each group.

## C.1 Origin, the global level and phase topology

**Origin / pre-layer:**

$$U_0 \rightsquigarrow \{c_0,\, C_m,\, c^+\}_{\text{simultaneous — together, in the same constitution}}, \qquad U_0 \notin \mathrm{Obj}(\mathcal{C}_{\rm faz})$$


**The whole of the system — sourced canon, meaning partly open:**

$$U_0\leftrightarrow[c_0\rightleftarrows C_m\rightleftarrows c^+]\equiv U_x,\qquad U_x\equiv\{c_0,C_m,c^+\}_{\rm sistem\ bütünü}$$

```latex
U_0\leftrightarrow[c_0\rightleftarrows C_m\rightleftarrows c^+]\equiv U_x,\qquad U_x\equiv\{c_0,C_m,c^+\}_{\rm sistem\ bütünü}
```

The chain notation here preserves the historical source expression; the current gate topology is the complete bidirectional triangle below. The full meaning of the relation $U_0\leftrightarrow U_x$ is an open debt.

**Phase topology (K3):**

$$c_0 \overset{\Delta_{0m}}{\longleftrightarrow} C_m, \qquad C_m \overset{\Delta_{+m}}{\longleftrightarrow} c^+, \qquad c_0 \overset{\Delta_{0+}}{\longleftrightarrow} c^+$$


**The global universe cycle, the two birth events and the safeguards:**

$$U_0 \;\leftrightarrow\; U_x \;\leftrightarrow\; U_+, \qquad U_0,\,U_x,\,U_+ \notin \{c_0,\,C_m,\,c^+\}$$

```latex

```

$$U_x \text{ crosses the critical threshold at the boundary of } U_0 \Rightarrow \text{Big Bang}, \qquad U_x \text{ crosses the critical threshold at the boundary of } U_+ \Rightarrow \text{Big Rupture}, \qquad \text{in both cases } \Rightarrow U_x$$

```latex
U_x \text{ crosses the critical threshold at the boundary of } U_0 \Rightarrow \text{Big Bang}, \qquad U_x \text{ crosses the critical threshold at the boundary of } U_+ \Rightarrow \text{Big Rupture}, \qquad \text{in both cases } \Rightarrow U_x
```

$$U_+\;=\;\text{Absolute Dissolution}\;\equiv\;\text{Absolute Rupture}\qquad(\text{when the whole } U_x \text{ structure reaches the form of } U_+)$$

```latex
U_+\;=\;\text{Absolute Dissolution}\;\equiv\;\text{Absolute Rupture}\qquad(\text{when the whole } U_x \text{ structure reaches the form of } U_+)
```

$$\mathrm{Genesis} = U_0 \rightsquigarrow \{c_0,C_m,c^+\}_{\text{simultaneous}}, \qquad \mathrm{CycleSeed} = \{\mathcal{L}_{\rm çöküş}, \mathcal{L}_{\rm seyrelme}\},\qquad \text{Cycle index} \neq t, \qquad U_0 : c_0 \;\not::\; U_+ : c^+$$


**The double-limit core seed — mechanism set aside for V24:**

$$\mathrm{DoubleLimit}_{\rm çekirdek}=\{\text{collapse / saturation limit},\ \text{dilution / closed-cycle limit}\},\qquad \mathrm{DoubleLimit}_{\rm çekirdek}\nRightarrow(c_0\to U_0)\ \text{as an ordinary phase transition}$$

## C.3 Time

$$dt=\Gamma\,\mathcal G_{C_m}\,d\tau,\qquad \tau\neq\text{fundamental physical time}$$

```latex
dt=\Gamma\,\mathcal G_{C_m}\,d\tau,\qquad \tau\neq\text{fundamental physical time}
```

# APPENDIX D — THOUGHT EXPERIMENTS AND ANALOGIES

The following are **conceptual tools**. None of them is evidence or a derivation.

## Informational compression

In c₀, a state is a complete and undifferentiated, metric-gapless single whole. In the transition to Cₘ, this whole is **read by being resolved**: at finite resolution, in the form of discrete objects. This is the intuitive picture of why Cₘ measurement has finite resolution.

## The dashboard

Cₘ is **the readable surface** of reality. A dashboard is not the engine itself; it is the readable state of the engine. But without the dashboard, nothing can be said about the engine.

## The cinema screen

The motion seen on the screen is not in the screen itself; it arises from reading successive frames. Time is like this too: not something that "flows", but a reading **that arises from accumulation**.

## Değnek Ambarı ("baton depot")

The name of the working method. An arrangement in which several independent tools work by auditing one another. None of them alone is authoritative; the value comes from **crossing**. The final decision is the author's.

## Collective behaviour

Bose–Einstein condensation and superfluidity are laboratory-accessible examples of discrete objects behaving like a collective whole. **The BEC is not c₀; it is the environment in the matter phase closest to the c₀ environment** (18.5). For this reason it is the best window for looking at the c₀ single-structure intuition from within Cₘ; but the window is not the thing it looks at.

## The resolver call

$X$ is not a value but **the result of a resolution call**: it takes the context and returns what is valid in that context. It is only a programming analogy.

## The Moon / informational-resolution analogy

The historical "Moon analogy" was used to separate the full ontological state from the finite readout capacity of Cₘ during readout between phases. The safe core of the analogy is: **the full state of a source and the Cₘ projection taken from it are not the same kind of information**. This is neither evidence of physical data compression nor a derivation of a particular number of bits.

## The Hartman effect

From the point of view of standard physics, the Hartman effect is the phenomenon of the group delay reaching saturation in opaque tunnelling barriers; in standard theory, this saturation is explained by the group delay being related not to a traversal time but to the release time of the energy stored in the barrier (Winful 2003). The Evertophy reading is therefore **a compatible reading**, not a verification. In Evertophy it motivates only the following thought experiment: in the phase-threshold region, the language of a pure Cₘ traversal time may be inadequate.

$$\boxed{\;\text{Hartman saturation} \neq \text{traversal time really zero} \neq \text{evidence of a faster-than-light signal}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{Hartman saturation} \neq \text{traversal time really zero} \neq \text{evidence of a faster-than-light signal}\;}
```

## The Schwinger effect

Schwinger pair production is the production of particle pairs from the energy of a strong external field. Under the identity c⁺ ≡ vacuum, this phenomenon is **read** as a local phase transition triggered by external energy on the c⁺ → Cₘ edge (candidate reading, 18.4). But:

$$\boxed{\;\text{The Schwinger effect} \neq \text{evidence of the ontological transition } c^+\to C_m\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{The Schwinger effect} \neq \text{evidence of the ontological transition } c^+\to C_m\;}
```

The energy does not come "from nothing": the energy of the external field enters the budget.

## The black-hole thought experiment

Black hole ≡ c₀ is the author's ontological definition; the one-to-one identity of the event horizon and the phase boundary, however, has not been established. Taking the density at its Planck value ($\rho_*=\rho_P$; the smallest radius), in a black hole of one solar mass the radius scale of the single structure ($\sim10^{-23}$ m) is much smaller than the horizon radius (~3 km). The current core preserves the following distinction:

```text
horizon = causal / geometric structure of Cₘ
c₀      = the pre-temporal phase of a metric-gapless integrated single structure
```

## The archive / library analogy

That records in an archive are accessible together does not show that the archive has its own "flowing time". This analogy conveys intuitively the separation of provenance order and physical time in c⁺.

## Historical processing analogies

**Registry saturation** is an interpretation that conveys the idea of archive saturation instead of heat death; it is neither a thermodynamic result nor a law of c⁺. The language of **IPW, processing jitter, frame rate** and **gearbox** carries the intuition of finite processing / sampling in the Cₘ reading; it produces no canonical "gear throughput" prediction for $H_0$. The expressions "c⁺ network / skeleton" and "bound / free texture" are likewise historical narrative faces; they do not replace the current definition of c⁺.

## Değnek Ambarı — a four-layer note on method

In the historical narrative, the Değnek Ambarı carries four separate layers of protection: physics audit, method audit, the risk of AI drift, and an ethics / consciousness lock. These layers are a working discipline; they are not physical phases or fundamental ontological elements.

## The satellite–electron chain of thought experiments

The satellite–electron chain of thought experiments: (1) the orbital electron — the very long-term behaviour of an electron in a pure space environment; (2) the projection of the universal Planck density. The aim of the chain is to convey an intuition of the laboratory–space relation of c₀; it is not a proposal for measurement.

## A thought exercise on the vacuum experiment

This is an exercise in how a process would be read in a perfect vacuum (cosmic or laboratory). In the current canon, **the vacuum is the Cₘ reading of c⁺** (c⁺ ≡ vacuum, Section 3); the vacuum state of quantum field theory is the computational language of this identity. The exercise recalls two things: that the vacuum looks "empty" is not the absence of c⁺ but the way it is read in Cₘ; and the absence of matter does not prove c₀ either, because c₀ is not the absence of matter but the state of a metric-gapless single structure.

## Table of analogies: BEC super-atom and superfluid helium-4

| Laboratory system | Contribution to the c₀ intuition |
|---|---|
| BEC super-atom | Collective behaviour in a single quantum state — the environment in the matter phase closest to c₀ |
| Superfluid He-4 | Frictionless collective flow |

Both are examples of the family of analogies of Section 3; they are not real identities.

## The frequency-transformation thought experiment

This is the analogy-level statement of the record of 25.32.

## "From a soft dial to a sudden switch" — an analogy of the phase regime–threshold boundary

This is an everyday analogy conveying the behaviour of the boundary band: the transition between a soft dial (gradual) and a sudden switch (threshold). The terminology lock of 25.1 also applies here — the everyday keyword does not enter the academic text.

# APPENDIX E — EPISTEMIC ORDER

## Who determines what

| Source | Authority |
|---|---|
| **Direct architect (author) clarification** | Canon — the status of source code |
| **Current core** | The ontology in force |
| **Current formal canon** | Verified formal result |
| **Compatible formal candidate** | Subject to approval |
| **Historical archive** | Preserved, not mixed in |
| **Tool interpretation** | Draft |

$$\boxed{\;\text{Rules of method bind the TOOLS; they do not bind the ARCHITECT.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{Rules of method bind the TOOLS; they do not bind the ARCHITECT.}\;}
```

If a rule says "do not produce new canonical content", it is directed at **the tools**.

## Items of direct canon

The following are direct ontological inputs of the architect and cannot be changed by tool interpretation:

- That $U_0$ forms the three phases **simultaneously** — together, in the same constitution
- The topology of the **complete bidirectional triangle**
- The internal ontological identity $c^+ \equiv$ dark matter $\equiv$ dark energy $\equiv$ **vacuum**
- That even the space between the particles that make up the atom is c⁺
- **Black hole ≡ c₀**, and that matter in c₀ is **in the form of a single structure**, contiguous to a degree that converges to the metric-gapless state at the Planck scale (which does not mean a Planck-sized core); that capacity is counted in Planck masses and that density does not exceed the Planck density
- That time in c₀ has not stopped but **has not yet been established**; that time in c⁺ is dissolved ($\bot_0\neq\bot_+\neq0$)
- That the kinematic membership band of Cₘ is $0<v<c$; that $v=c$ is the liminal shared boundary of Cₘ and c⁺
- That **the record channel is unidirectional in the Cₘ → c⁺ direction** and that, once sealed, the record remains fixed
- That the BEC is not c₀ but **the environment in the matter phase closest to the c₀ environment**
- That c⁺ is a **closed cycle**, and that its justification is **first the academic boundary**
- The core status of **the method of the phase-parameter matrix**
- That $[P,P]$ is a valid range
- **The existence of the global universe level** ($U_0$, $U_x$, $U_+$) and its separateness from the phase level
- **$U_+$ — Absolute Dissolution ≡ Absolute Rupture**; the Big Rupture is related to the critical threshold at the boundary of $U_+$
- **That the Dynamic Universe may or may not come to an end**
- **The Principle of Silence**
- **The third principle:** a mathematical representation that correctly reproduces a measurement is accepted unconditionally within the scope of that measurement; there is no ontological priority among representations
- **The core-structure status of the canonical figure** (APPENDIX J)

## How novelty works

A later record does not invalidate an earlier one — it **writes over it**, and the old one is kept together with the reason why it was abandoned. The learning history of a framework is as informative as its present state.

## Summary of tool alignment

Every tool working with this monograph must know these four things:

1. **The order of precedence** is in the table above; a tool's own output is at the bottom.
2. **Historical content is preserved but not mixed into the current core.**
3. **Assignment of mechanism is not derivation**; writing a correspondence does not produce a number.
4. **The counter is at 0/10**, and it does not move unless the six conditions are satisfied together.

# APPENDIX F — FORMAL ARCHITECTURE AND STATE OF IMPLEMENTATION

## Layers

The aim of the formal core is not to compress all physical content into a single structure, but to separate semantic classes at the level of types. When the type system enforces the layer to which a concept belongs, most of the warnings in the text that "must be kept separate" become errors that the compiler catches by itself.

```text
Origin / upper layer:
    U₀
    Uₓ = holistic representation of the three-phase system
    U₊ = Absolute Dissolution ≡ Absolute Rupture

Ontology:
    Phase = {c₀, Cₘ, c⁺}
    X = meta-resolver (not a scalar; its readings ξ_k = π_k(X))

Gates:
    complete bidirectional triangle (Δ₀ₘ, Δ₊ₘ stamped; Δ₀₊ stampless)

Transition:
    Edge class + direction + admissibility

Stamp:
    Stamp class + stamp emission + monoid M

Archive:
    raw provenance records + append-only order
    record channel: Cₘ → c⁺ (unidirectional; seal fixed)

Coordination:
    c₀↔c⁺ X-relation / fibre candidate

Readout:
    Cₘ observables (including the c⁺ ≡ vacuum reading)

Method:
    Parameter Budget + Bridge Budget + preregistration
```

## Minimal type skeleton

The following scheme is an orientation for implementation; it is not the final programming language of the ontology.

```lean
inductive Phase
  | c0
  | cm
  | cp

inductive TemporalRole
  | preEmergent
  | physicalTime
  | atemporalRecord

inductive EdgeClass
  | e0m
  | emp
  | e0p

inductive ArchiveEffect
  | noMutation
  | append
  | readProject

inductive CoordEffect
  | none
  | xCoord

structure TransitionSemantics where
  src : Phase
  dst : Phase
  edge : EdgeClass
  stampEmission : Option Stamp
  archiveEffect : ArchiveEffect
  coordEffect : CoordEffect
```

`U₀`, `Uₓ` and `X` are not constructors of the type `Phase`. `Uₓ` is the representation, at the level of the whole system, of the three `Phase` constructors; it adds no new fourth constructor.

## Priorities of formal closure

1. For the normal-form reducer / rewriting system, that all paths meet in the same normal form (confluence) — **closed** (14.2).
2. General associativity — **closed** (14.2).
3. Classification of idempotents — **closed** (14.2); what remains is only package integration.
4. The full range character for $I(\mathbb Q)$ / the real numbers.
5. Uniqueness of the archive `rid` and the append-only invariants.
6. Integration of $q$ / $\Sigma$ / the meaning of archive direction (aligned with the ruling of the record channel Cₘ → c⁺).
7. A minimal type and Parameter Budget accounting for the $X$-coordination relation / fibre.
8. The formal type of the interfaces for Cₘ metric reconstruction.
9. Encoding $\bot_0$ and $\bot_+$ as separate types (`undefined0` / `dissolvedPlus`), and separating the pedagogical shorthand $dt_{c^+}=0$ from the numerical zero at the type level.
10. Splitting the meaning of direction into separate types: `TransitionDirection`, `StampCarried`, `StampEmitted`, `ArchiveAppend` (13.3, 14.7).
11. Typing the global boundary relations (`GlobalBoundary(Uₓ,U₀)`, `Genesis`, `Dissolution`) as pure relations containing no time parameter.
12. Encoding the band types ($B_{\rm kesin}$, $B_{1-\alpha}$, $B_{\rm inanç}$) as separate types (21.6).
13. Publication package: packaging the canonical figure file (`Evertophy_Core_20260919_revize_gerekiyor_v2.png`) with the document; a reproducible build record with the Lean / toolchain version, source digest and axiom inventory.

## Interpreting the number of theorems

The 119 theorem declarations reported in the earlier inventory (historical inventory; current inventory $269 = 46$ production $+ 223$ archive, 23.10) show the machine-checked part of formal definitions and invariants. This number does **not** mean: 119 independent laws of physics · 119 experimental successes · the verification of Evertophy.

$$269=46_{\rm üretim}+223_{\rm arşiv},\qquad 223=66_{\rm eski}+53_{\rm V03}+104_{\rm V24}$$

```latex
269=46_{\rm üretim}+223_{\rm arşiv},\qquad 223=66_{\rm eski}+53_{\rm V03}+104_{\rm V24}
```

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| **What does it do?** | It breaks down numerically the composition of the formal inventory |
| **Why does it do it?** | To prevent the number of theorems from being read as a success of physics, and to make the consistency of the count visible |
| **How does it do it?** | It splits the total into production and archive, and the archive into three subpackages |
| **What result is expected?** | That the same number is obtained again by an independent external build |
| **What result is obtained?** | **It is a formal inventory**; it is not a count of physical verifications. The count is recorded with attribution to the source run (23.10) |

## Current formal status

**Formal / production core — closed:** the distinction between phase and global node · that $U_x$ is not a pole · the phase-indexed value type · the admissibility of the metric only in Cₘ · kind of quantity ≠ dimension · native / source / observation phase roles · the typed Cₘ readout interface · gate ≠ transport permission · the Cₘ-specific gate lock · gate lock ⟺ at most one native phase · the append monotonicity of the archive count · the distinctions of event kind and trace policy · associativity, termination, unique normal form, confluence and classification of idempotents of the stamp algebra · the non-uniqueness of the bridge in a finite model.

**Formal candidate / open:** the physical readout law · the general bridge selector · the realization of the $X$-fibre · metric reconstruction · fully general, extended and dependency-tracking range arithmetic · non-production path and category candidates · encoding the capacity relations of the c₀ single structure ($N_{c_0}=M/m_P$, $\rho\le\rho_P$) at the type level.

## Typed ontology extension — Phase / GlobalNode, Gate / GlobalRel, Ux ∉ Pole

Formal proposal: the two ontological levels are separated at the type level — `Phase` (c₀, Cₘ, c⁺) and `GlobalNode` (U₀, Uₓ, U₊); gates are of type `Gate`, global arrows of type `GlobalRel`, and the invariant `Ux ∉ Pole` is enforced at the type level. **Closed** in the production core: the distinction between phase and global node, that $U_x$ is not a pole, the phase-indexed value type, the Cₘ-specificity of the metric, kind of quantity ≠ dimension, gate ≠ transport permission, phase roles and the gate lock. Outside production and **open**: the full algebra of global relations, the path-groupoid extension, the physical bridge selector, the $X$-fibre and metric reconstruction.

## Value types — CmOnlyValue, MetricAdmissible, QuantityKind / Dimension

**Status: formal — closed (production core).** Family of types: stamping Cₘ-specific quantities with `CmOnlyValue`, the condition `MetricAdmissible` for the metric, separating the kind of quantity from the dimension label (the formal counterpart of 5.3a).

## Bridge types — BridgeSpec, CmReadoutBridge, the non-uniqueness of the bridge

**Status: non-uniqueness formally closed; the general selector open.** The formal statement of the proven result of non-uniqueness (24.1): several `BridgeSpec`s can satisfy the same conditions of type and well-definedness; therefore the selection of the bridge requires observation.

## Gate typing — the typed gate, GateRelated ≠ TransportPermission

**Status: formal — closed (production core).** The formal counterpart of 13.1a: while `GateRelated(c0, cp)` is true, this gives no permission to transport a quantity; transport permission is of a separate type `phase-license`.

## Path algebra — ordinary category / path groupoid and a staged non-scalar X + ξ projections

The formal background of 13.2: composition of transitions in an ordinary category, an invertible path algebra in the bare layer (path groupoid), a monoid in the stamp layer (not a group). For the non-scalar $X$, the edge-specific readout projections $\xi_{ij}=\pi_{ij}(X)$.

```text
assert Range(P,P) is valid
```

These tests are not physical evidence; they are **a safeguard of ontological compatibility**.

# APPENDIX J — CANONICAL RENDER (THE CORE VISUAL STRUCTURE)

This appendix carries the canonical figure of the framework and its source code. The figure is not an illustration but **the core structure itself**: details may change in later versions; the structure does not.

## J.1 How to read the figure

| Element | Ontological meaning |
|---|---|
| Left red circle — $U_0$ (Absolute Core) | The global saturation pole; the pre-layer in which all the structures of the Dynamic Universe exist in a metric-gapless single form |
| Left curly bracket | The boundary between the global level and the phase system |
| Black frame — $U_x$ at the top, *Dynamic Universe* at the bottom | The global structure in which the three phases are realized together |
| Three thick black dots | The phase nodes: $c_0$, $C_m$, $c^+$ |
| Three bidirectional arrows — $\Delta_{0m}$, $\Delta_{0+}$, $\Delta_{+m}$ | The complete bidirectional triangle: three gates; $\Delta_{0m}$ and $\Delta_{+m}$ carry stamps, $\Delta_{0+}$ is stampless |
| Right curly bracket | The same boundary, on the side of dissolution |
| Right red circle — $U_+$ (Absolute Dissolution ≡ Absolute Rupture) | The global dissolution pole |
| Thick outer arrows | **Relation with the whole** (birth / limit) — not phase gates |

## J.2 Invariant core elements

1. **Two levels:** the outer poles ($U_0$, $U_+$) and the inner phase system ($U_x$)
2. **Symmetry:** $U_0$ on the left, $U_+$ on the right, with equal visual weight
3. **The complete bidirectional triangle** and its three labelled edges
4. **Thick black dots at the vertices** — they separate the phase node from the boundary band
5. **The outer arrows end at the brackets** — they touch no phase point
6. **The frame is labelled $U_x$ / Dynamic Universe**

## J.3 Details that may change

Label texts and subtitles · colour tones and font sizes · fine adjustment of coordinates · auxiliary layers (representation of the phase regime–threshold boundary, a speed band under $C_m$, the wavelength factor, the black-hole threshold factor).

## J.4 Semantic safeguards

$$\boxed{\;\text{At the global level, the symbol } \leftrightarrow \text{ does not claim an INVERTIBLE OPERATOR.}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{At the global level, the symbol } \leftrightarrow \text{ does not claim an INVERTIBLE OPERATOR.}\;}
```

Reading from left to right gives the impression of a process; **the cycle is not a flow of time** (Section 2.8). In the figure, the phase nodes and the global poles **do not belong to the same graphical class**: the nodes are black dots, the poles are red circles — this distinction must be preserved.

## J.5 Canonical LaTeX / TikZ source code

In the Turkish source, the code is preserved exactly in its minor12 form, as required by the double-sealing protocol (23.10a). In this English version, the drawing commands, coordinates and styles are identical to that source; only the comments and the node label strings have been translated into English, so that the rendered figure carries English labels.

# APPENDIX K — OPEN DEBTS AND PENDING DECISIONS

## K.1 Frozen open debts

These are not history; they are the real present limits of the ontology:

- **The general physical selector** — the principle that makes unique the bridge from $X$ to a physical quantity ($J_X$, $\mathcal E_\phi$).
- **The formal definition of the projection operation of the Force of Attraction** ($\Pi$) and **the equation of balance of the three phases**.
- **The projection form of repulsion and of charge** (electromagnetism); the projection account of the strong and weak forces.
- The quantitative mechanism **from c⁺ to dark matter and dark energy**; under the identity **c⁺ ≡ vacuum**, the quantitative bridge between vacuum energy and the observed dark-energy density (the cosmological constant problem).
- **The quantitative map between entropy and sealing.**
- The **membership reading** $\mathcal R_v$ of $v$ and the **mathematical representation** of c⁺ being beyond c; the formal type of the liminal shared boundary $v=c$.
- The exact type and physical projection of $X$ · the formal definition of the saturation reading $\xi_{\rm sat}=\pi_{\rm sat}(X)$ · the physical readout law · the reconstruction of the metric from the ontology.
- Whether the number of phases is fundamental or derived · the structural type of $U_x$ and the laws of the global cycle.
- The Standard Model, Yukawa and QCD bridges · a preregistered empirical prediction.
- **Recovery** of the observational results of general relativity from the effective 3+1 metric.
- Recovery, in the quantum reading, of Born probabilities, interference, Bell correlations, contextuality and no-signalling.
- A general guarantee of **algorithmic convergence** for $X$.
- Uniqueness of record identity in the concatenation of raw archives; aligning the formal stamp direction ($\kappa_{+m}$) with the record direction fixed as Cₘ → c⁺ by the author's ruling (7.1, 13.3).
- The quantitative and observational map of the identity black hole ≡ c₀: the relation between event horizon and phase boundary, the threshold and transition law of the mass channel.
- **The internal profile of the c₀ single structure and its quantitative relation with the event horizon:** whether the density is constant at the Planck limit or has a profile; the observational consequences of the ratio $R_{c_0}/R_s\propto M^{-2/3}$.
- The quantitative form of the dependence of c₀ capacity on mass and area; the canonical language of the separation between entropy (with area) and capacity (with mass) (3.2).
- The quantitative form of the rule of departure from c₀ (speed and / or density), and the distribution of the capacity that decreases in a merger (3.2).
- Expressing with a quantitative measure of closeness the fact that the BEC is "the environment in the matter phase closest to the c₀ environment".
- An upper bound from entropy production on the subatomic cycle rate under the direct c₀ ⇄ c⁺ edge lock — model (13.6).
- The c₀ readout map $\mathcal O^{C_m}_{c_0}$ (the effective Cₘ volume, radius and density parameters of the single structure) and the derivation of the constant density $\rho_*$ (3.2).
- The type and measure of the phase-boundary axis $q_\partial$; its relation to the kinematic membership reading $\beta$ (18.0).
- The sector-specific form of $\Pi$ ($\Pi_a:\mathcal A\to\mathcal L_a$, a sector-specific category of effective action or equation) and the recovery of gauge invariance, spin / chirality and fermion mass.
- The families of general-relativistic solutions covered by the effective-geometry map $g^{\rm eff}_{\mu\nu}[E^3,X,\Delta]$ (including families that do not admit a flat spatial slicing; 9.2a).
- The physical selector, for each context, of the c⁺ readout map $\mathrm{Okuma}_{C_m}(c^+;\mathcal C)$ (11.1a).

Partly established: full range arithmetic · inverse problems with dependent variables · the source derivation of some model families · a full Morse range certificate.

$$\boxed{\;\text{OPEN} \;\neq\; \text{FAILED}\;}$$

```latex
\boxed{\;\text{OPEN} \;\neq\; \text{FAILED}\;}
```

## K.2 Awaiting the author's decision

**Closed decisions (earlier versions):** the base space of Cₘ is flat three-dimensional Euclidean space, and time emerges (9.2a); the publication package consists of the Core Ontology, the latest version of V24 and the final compilation of the marathon; the black hole and the c₀ phase are ontologically identical (3.2) · c₀ is not absolute zero, and temperature is undefined in c₀ (3.2) · time exists only in Cₘ and is unidirectional; the record channel is in the Cₘ → c⁺ direction (7.1) · a black hole is neither an annihilation nor an infinite singularity (3.2) · the phases are not derived from Cₘ (2.4) · the model rule for the direct c₀ ⇄ c⁺ edge (13.6).

**Decisions closed in this version (author approvals of 2026-10-14):**

- The kinematic membership band of Cₘ is $0<v<c$; $v=c$ is the liminal shared boundary of Cₘ and c⁺ (boundary ≠ membership).
- **c⁺ ≡ dark matter ≡ dark energy ≡ vacuum**; the vacuum of quantum field theory is the Cₘ computational language of this identity; the statement "c⁺ ≠ vacuum" has been removed.
- Even the space between the particles that make up the atom is c⁺.
- Time in c⁺ is dissolved: $\bot_+$ is the ontological state, $dt_{c^+}=0$ only a pedagogical shorthand; $\bot_0\neq\bot_+\neq0$.
- The saturation of c₀ is written not through $X$ itself but through its reading $\xi_{\rm sat}=\pi_{\rm sat}(X)\to\xi_{\rm sat}^{\max}$.
- **The reading of a "gapless core of Planck density" has been closed:** matter in c₀ is **in the form of a contiguous, finite single structure**, to a degree that converges to the metric-gapless state at the Planck scale; this does not mean "the core is Planck-sized". Capacity is counted in Planck masses ($N_{c_0}=M/m_P$), density cannot exceed the Planck density ($\rho_{c_0}\le\rho_P$), volume grows with mass ($V_{c_0}\ge N_{c_0}\ell_P^3$); exact proportionality of volume to mass, and $R_{c_0}\propto M^{1/3}$, hold in the constant-density realization ($\rho_{c_0}=\rho_*$). The smallest radius given for one solar mass is calculated by taking the density at its Planck value ($\rho_*=\rho_P$). That density does not increase with mass is not derived from the upper bound but is a separate ruling; $V$, $R$, $\rho$ are effective Cₘ readout parameters (3.2). It is not additionally claimed that the density is locked exactly to the Planck value; there is only the upper bound (3.2, 25.28).
- Time in c₀ **has not stopped; it has not yet been established**; the summary language of "zero differential" has been dropped.
- When the whole $U_x$ structure reaches the form of $U_+$, **Absolute Rupture and Absolute Dissolution express the same thing**.
- Retired criteria remain retired (for example, $\nabla\ln\chi_S\neq0$ for dark matter).
- The sealing cost $C_s$ is in the "candidate reading" mode.
- Readings of experiments (delayed-choice quantum eraser, teleportation, etc.) are in the "compatible reading" mode; teleportation requires a classical channel, and c⁺ is the source of the correlation.
- The BEC is not c₀; it is the environment in the matter phase closest to the c₀ environment.
- The third principle: a mathematical representation that correctly reproduces a measurement is accepted unconditionally within the scope of that measurement; there is no ontological priority among representations.

**Still pending:**

- Whether the archive count is to be defined as the record count of the raw archive · acceptance of the monotonicity of phase memory as an architectural rule.
- The type of the relation between SYNTAX and $U_x$.
- The fate of the channel-asymmetry bridge · confirmation of the source text of some model families.
- Canonization of the Hawking temperature as the Cₘ readout temperature of the horizon (3.2).
- Harmonization of $\Pi_{+\to m}$ and of the historical ruling "necessarily through c₀" with the canon of the complete triangle (23.10b).

## K.3 Classes of confidence

In this document, the confidence with which each statement is made is indicated in the sentence: **canon** (a ruling approved by the author) · **author's ruling** (a direct clarification of the author) · **formally proven** (within the scope of the encoded system) · **model** (correct within its own assumptions) · **bridge** (a candidate connection between the core and observation) · **candidate reading** / **compatible reading** · **open** · **retired**. The row "What result is obtained?" following each equation tells the reader directly the confidence class of that equation.
# APPENDIX L — TRANSLATION KEY (ENGLISH VERSION ONLY)

This appendix exists only in the English version; the Turkish source has no counterpart. It adds no ontological content. Its task is to make the two versions **comparable line by line**: it fixes the canonical English terms in a single table, explains the Turkish labels that remain inside the formulas, and lists everything the English version adds editorially.

## L.1 Locked terminology

The following equivalents are canonical. A later English minor may not replace them with synonyms without the author's ruling. Symbols ($c_0$, Cₘ, c⁺, $U_0$, $U_x$, $U_+$, $X$, $\xi_k$, $\Delta_{0m}$, $\Delta_{+m}$, $\Delta_{0+}$, $\bot_0$, $\bot_+$) are never translated.

Two rules of application:

- **The lock holds in every typographic form.** A locked term keeps the same English word when it is capitalized for emphasis inside a boxed ruling (for example INTERVAL-VALUED, UNIDIRECTIONAL).
- **Range is a name; interval-valued is a description.** *Range* is the name of the formalism and of the type (Range Formalism, $\mathrm{Aralık}$, $[P,P]$ as a valid range). *Interval-valued* describes a quantity or a syntax whose value is a range. The two are not interchangeable.

| Turkish (source) | English (canonical) | Note |

# CLOSING

Evertophy is a framework which proposes that reality consists of **two ontological levels**: inside, the dynamic universe constituted by three phases; outside, the two limiting conditions of that universe. Between the phases, a complete bidirectional triangle; above them all, a single dynamic meta-resolver; and before them all, an unnumbered premise — that the Force of Attraction is the fundamental ontological postulate.

This document aimed to set out completely the **ontological skeleton** of the framework. It does not contain full bodies of proof; it summarizes the results and the provenance of formal proofs. What it chiefly offers is a conceptual order. In this version that order has been made one step more explicit: each equation has been presented to the reader together with its symbols and with the questions "what does it do, why does it do it, how does it do it, what result is expected, what result is obtained"; in this way what a formula **does not say** has become as visible as what it says.

Five things must be said plainly.

**First:** The framework is mathematically incomplete. The central chain from $X$ to physical functions has not been written. This incompleteness has not been hidden; it is kept on record in the framework's own accounting. The full mathematical realization of $X$ **has been deliberately left open**.

**Second:** The framework has no active claim of empirical evidence. The line once regarded as the strongest was closed **by internal audit**. This closure is itself the framework's most trustworthy behaviour: a claim was refuted not by an external referee but by its own protocol.

**Third:** The global level is the newest and least defined layer. The function sentence of $U_+$, the mathematical type of the global arrows and the link of the cycle are open. About the direct relation between the two poles, the framework deliberately **remains silent** — because passing judgement on something that has no representation would mean leaving the line of observation and experiment.

**Fourth:** The present value of the framework lies not in the numbers it produces but **in the order it establishes and the prohibitions it lays down**. A framework is valuable not according to how much it explains, but according to how clearly it **becomes falsifiable**.

**Fifth:** The formal results in this text are valid within the scope of the encoded system; the text as a whole has passed through cross-audit, but cross-audit is not proof. The audit showed that the skeleton is consistent at the level of types and algebra, weeded out more than thirty claims and located the central gap definitively. The ontological changes in this version — c⁺ ≡ vacuum, the c⁺ reading of the space inside the atom, the metric-gapless single structure of c₀, the band $0<v<c$ and the liminal boundary $v=c$ — are rulings not of the tools but of **the author**. Passing an audit does not mean being right; but knowing where one may be wrong is the first step on the way to what is right.

Evertophy is at present not a theory completed to this degree, but **an ontology and physics research programme that aims at a Theory of Everything**. The internal status of this version is **Stable Closure Candidate / Current Core Reference**: it is open to direct revision by the architect, and reserved layers such as the cycle mechanism of V24 have not yet been closed.
